みずほ経済 EXPRESS

中国が政策金利を翌日物に変更 
～銀行金利市場化の長い道のり～

調査部 主任エコノミスト 月岡直樹 
080-1069-6684 naoki.tsukioka@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ 人民銀がリバースレポ翌日物の日常オペを開始。短期金利の誘導精度を高める狙い 
中国の中央銀行に当たる中国人民銀行（PBOC）が10月30日、市場に短期流動性を供給する「売り戻
し条件付き債券購入（リバースレポ）」翌日物の公開市場操作（オペ）を11月2日より毎営業日実施す
ると発表した。これまで原則として毎営業日実施していたリバースレポ7日物から切り替える。翌日物
のオペは固定金利、数量入札の方式で行い、その金利を政策金利と位置づける。インターバンク市場
のレポ取引では翌日物が大半を占めていることから、政策金利を7日物から翌日物に変更することで
期間のズレを解消し、短期市場金利の誘導精度を高める狙いがある。新たなオペ初日の金利は1.25％
であった（前営業日のリバースレポ7日物の金利は1.40％）。 
この動きは、PBOCの潘功勝行長（総裁）が10月19日の講演で明らかにしていた金融政策運営の改革
方針を実行に移したものである。潘氏は「銀行間債券担保付レポ翌日物金利（DR001）は事実上、中
央銀行の金融政策が調節する目標金利となっているが、それと同時に、中央銀行の公開市場操作の主
要な種類と主要な政策金利の期限はなお7日で、両者は完全には一致しない」ことを認めた上で、「適
切なタイミングで公開市場の主要な操作種類の期限を翌日物に切り替え、公開市場の翌日物レポ金利
を主要な政策金利とすることを検討している」と予告していた。この切り替えによって、PBOCの金融
政策運営は米連邦準備制度理事会（FRB）や日本銀行のそれに近づくことになる。 
さらに、潘氏は銀行貸出金利の改革を進めていることも明らかにした。最優遇貸出金利（LPR）を
参照して貸出金利を決定するこれまでの運用に加え、DR001などの短期市場金利を直接参照して金利
を決定する方法を試験的に導入したのである。今後、財務力のある企業は商業銀行と協議の上でDR001
もしくはLPRを参照した貸出金利を選択することができ、小規模企業や個人には引き続きLPRを参照
した金利を適用するとしている。経済誌『財新』によれば、2026年7月以降にDR001を参照した銀行貸
出がすでに全国で100件以上実施されているという。 
PBOCが新たな銀行貸出金利の決定メカニズムを模索する背景には、LPRが「最優遇」から乖離し、
貸出金利の実勢を十分に反映できていないことがあるとみられる。これは、2013年7月に下限規制が撤
廃される前の貸出基準金利が基準の70％まで引き下げを許容していた歴史的経緯から、信用力の高い
顧客にLPR以下の金利で貸出を実行する慣行が残っているためでもある。潘氏は改革を進めると同時
に、すでに実務的な役割を終えている貸出基準金利と預金基準金利を正式に廃止するとした。 
2026 年 11 月 9 日

### Page 2

2

■ 貸出基準金利から最優遇貸出金利へ移行。ただ、実勢金利は「最優遇」から乖離 
PBOCは2010年代以降、銀行金利の市場化改革を慎重に推し進めてきた（図表1）。改革以前はPBOC
が貸出基準金利と預金基準金利を直接コントロールし、商業銀行がその上下一定の範囲内で貸出・預
金金利を決定する制度となっていたが、PBOCは2013年7月に貸出金利の下限規制を撤廃し、同年10月
には全国銀行間コール市場センター（全国銀行間同業拆借中心。以下「コール市場センター」）を通
じて貸出基礎金利（旧LPR）の発表を開始した。旧LPRは、主要9行（のち10行）の報告銀行がコール
市場センターに貸出の最優遇金利を報告し、センター側がそのうち最高値と最低値を除いた報告金利
を報告銀行の貸出残高で加重平均して算出するものであった。 
PBOCは銀行貸出金利が市場原理に基づき決定されるメカニズムへの移行を意図していたが、PBOC
が引き続き基準金利を操作して利下げを行い、報告銀行も基準金利を強く意識して最優遇金利を報告
したため、旧LPRは貸出基準金利に張り付いて変動した。PBOCは「銀行が貸出を実行する際、多くが
なお貸出基準金利を参照して金利を決定し、特に一部の銀行が協調行動を通じて貸出基準金利の一定
図表1 銀行金利の市場化改革 
年月 No. 改革内容 
2004/10  銀行貸出金利の上限規制と銀行預金金利の下限規制を撤廃 
2013/7  ① 銀行貸出金利の下限規制を撤廃（撤廃前の下限は貸出基準金利の 70％） 
2013/10 ② 貸出基礎金利（旧 LPR）の発表を開始 
2015/10 ③ 銀行預金金利の上限規制を撤廃（撤廃前の上限は預金基準金利の 150％） 
2019/8 ④ 貸出市場報告金利（新 LPR）の発表を開始。中期貸出ファシリティ（MLF）金利を参照金利に採用 
2024/7 [1] レポおよびリバースレポ翌日物の臨時オペを開始（金利はリバースレポ 7 日物の▲0.2％と＋0.5％） 
 ⑤ リバースレポ 7 日物金利を LPR の参照金利に位置づけ 
2026/6 [2] レポおよびリバースレポ翌日物の臨時オペを変更（金利はリバースレポ 7 日物の±0.25％） 
2026/11 ⑥ リバースレポ翌日物の日常的なオペを開始。その金利を政策金利に位置づけ

倍率（例えば0.9倍）を隠れた下限金利として設定しており、市場金利が実体経済に伝達することを妨
げている」と指摘している。 
そこで、PBOCは2019年8月、貸出市場報告金利（新LPR）と呼ばれる新たなメカニズムを導入する。
中長期流動性の供給ツールである中期貸出ファシリティ（MLF）の金利を参照し、銀行がこれに資金
調達コストや市場需給、リスクプレミアムを反映した最優遇金利をコール市場センターに報告する方
法である。報告銀行は中小銀行を追加して18行（現在20行）まで拡大し、算出方法も加重平均から単
純平均に変更したことで、算出結果がウェイトの高い大手行の報告金利に引っ張られる傾向を排し、
指標としての包括性も高まった。この改革により、LPRは政策金利であるMLF1年物の金利と連動する
ようになったが、LPRが「最優遇」金利から乖離した状態は依然として解消されなかった。報告銀行
が旧LPRの時と同様にMLF金利を強く意識して金利を報告したためと考えられる。 
図表3は新LPRと実際の銀行貸出金利との関係を見たものである。LPRを下回る金利での貸出比率が
年々高まっていることが分かる。LPR1年物は2019年8月～2026年10月までの間に計▲1.25％Pt引き下げ
られているが、実勢金利は資金需要の低迷もあってそれ以上に下がっていることを示唆している。 
一方、銀行預金金利は2015年に上限規制を撤廃した後も、銀行間の過度な預金獲得競争を防ぐため
窓口指導や業界の自主規制の形をとった上限管理を維持し、2022年4月になって10年物国債利回りや
LPR1年物を参照して金利を決定するメカニズムを導入した。上限管理が廃止されたわけではないが、
預金金利は近年、市場金利の低下に伴って低下傾向にある。 
■ 市場化改革の成果を銀行の実際の金利設定行動に定着させられるかが今後の焦点に 
PBOCは、2019年頃までに政策金利をリバースレポ7日物金利（短期）と MLF（中長期）の 2本立てと
する金融政策運営の枠組みを整え、新LPRの導入でその枠組みを明確化した（月岡（2024）参照）。
2024年7月には、政策金利をリバースレポ7日物に一本化し、LPRの参照金利もMLFからこれに移行さ
せた。中長期金利を市場の決定に委ねる形へと歩を進めたのである。 
PBOCは同時に、誘導目標とする短期金利を一定の範囲内で安定させるため金利コリドー（回廊）の

4

仕組みも整える（図表4）。2024年7月、流動性を吸収するレポと供給するリバースレポの翌日物の臨
時オペを開始し、レポ金利をリバースレポ7日物の▲0.2％Pt、リバースレポ金利を7日物の＋0.5％Ptに
設定することでコリドーの幅を狭めた。さらに、2026年6月には臨時オペの金利をそれぞれ▲0.25％Pt、
＋0.25％Ptに調整し、誘導目標とするDR001が臨時オペ金利の上下限を継続的に逸脱した場合にオペを
実施すると明確化した。リバースレポ翌日物の日常的なオペを実施する準備を整えた形である。 
このように、PBOCは政策金利による短期市場金利の誘導精度を高めるとともに、市場金利の変化を
実際の銀行貸出金利へと円滑に波及させるメカニズムの構築を進めてきた。今回、政策金利をリバー
スレポ翌日物金利に変更し、LPRを介さずにDR001を直接参照する貸出金利の決定方式を導入したこ
とは、一連の市場化改革における重要な節目と位置づけることができる。DR001参照型の金利は指標
金利としてのLPRを代替するものではなく、PBOCは両者を併存させながら金利波及経路を改善するア
プローチを続けるとみられる。今後は、市場化改革の成果を商業銀行の実際の金利設定行動に定着さ
せられるかが焦点となろう。

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國 9 月通膨數據高於市場預期，CPI 月增 0.6%、年增 3.7%；核心 CPI 月增
0.3%、年增 2.6%。同月 PPI 月增 1.3%，創 2022 年 3 月以來最大增幅，年增
6.3%，顯示通膨壓力已逐步由能源價格擴散至更廣泛領域。數據公布後，市場一
度提高對聯準會 10 月升息的預期，但隨後多位官員釋出審慎訊號，使升息押注
降溫。聯準會理事鮑曼表示，決策前仍有時間觀察先前升息效果；舊金山聯準銀
行總裁戴莉則認為 10 月無立即升息之必要。而褐皮書顯示，美國就業市場維持
「低招聘、低裁員」格局，而高能源價格帶來的通膨壓力仍值得關注。
⚫ 中國 9 月通膨持續回升，CPI 年增 0.9%，創近三個月高點；PPI 年增 5.0%，創
逾四年最大漲幅，顯示通縮壓力進一步減輕。主要受能源與原物料價格上漲帶
動，且 9 月進出口表現優於預期，AI 產業鏈需求與貿易環境改善提供支撐；不

過，信貸增長仍仰賴政府發債與季底效應，內需與民間融資動能仍偏弱。
⚫ 日本銀行審議委員小枝淳子表示，不宜對升息過於保守，並認為政策利率仍有進
一步上調空間。總裁植田和男則強調維持 2%通膨目標的重要性。整體而言，日
本央行持續釋出偏鷹派訊號，有助支撐日圓表現。
⚫ 歐洲央行管委溫施表示，在能源價格波動下，為了控制通膨仍不排除進一步升息
可能，總裁拉加德則認為法國債務問題應透過財政政策解決，歐洲央行官員普遍
認為能源價格的潛在波動依然是推升通膨並使其居高不下的主要風險來源。。
⚫ 澳洲央行會議紀錄指出，能源價格上漲與需求韌性可能使通膨更具黏性，保留
進一步升息空間。然而 9 月就業數據意外疲弱，市場大幅下調升息預期，目前
預估 11 月維持利率不變的機率接近 90%。

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
美元兌新台幣匯率走勢
Oct/19/2026
＜上週走勢＞
上週台幣匯價高點為 31.890，低點在 32.520，以 32.338 作收，較前一周收盤價
貶值 4 角 6.3 分。
上週台幣走貶，周初，外資延續前周大幅匯出的走勢，雖逢高吸引出口商拋匯，
仍不敵外資匯出力道令台幣走貶；週二台股重挫近千點，外資大幅賣超台股 1653 億
並匯出，台幣接連跌破 32.000、32.100 及 32.200，總成交量 71.3 億，創歷史新高；
周三台股收漲，外資雙向進出，台幣收斂部分跌幅；週四，受美國通膨高於預期影
響，早盤台幣跳空開低，台股跌逾 2%，外資大幅賣超 3025 億，台幣跌破 31.400 關
卡，總成交量 91.2 億，再創歷史新高；週五外資持續匯出，台幣貶至周內低點 32.520，
終場在央行調節之下收在 32.338。
＜未來分析＞
預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。就國際市場而言，美國 CPI、PPI 雙雙高於預期，通

膨壓力仍在，儘管聯準會官員陸續發言表示不急於升息，淡化市場對於聯準會十月
升息預期，惟年底前聯準會仍有升息一碼空間，利率持穩於高位將給予國際美元支
撐；就台灣市場來看，市場對 AI 產業資本支出、融資成本及信用風險產生疑慮，若
賣壓延續，將壓抑市場風險偏好，增加外資調整台股部位及匯出的壓力，令台幣面
臨持續貶值的壓力，惟出口商拋匯將抑制台幣貶值速度，綜合以上，預估本週台幣
走勢偏弱。
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.100-32.700

術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY 匯價目前交投於月線及年線之間，各天期均線高度
糾結，多空雙方於 158 關卡附近拉鋸，在有效突破季線及年線反壓或跌破月線支撐

殖利率呈區間格局的可能性較高，關注下週 10 年券標售結果，預估 5 年券區
間為 1.85%~1.90%，10 年券區間為 1.98%~2.03%。

2026 年 10 月 30 日

調査レポート

2026 年 7～9 月期の GDP（1 次速報）予測

～ほぼゼロ成長も、緩やかな景気回復は継続～

調査部 主任研究員 藤田 隼平

2026 年 11 月 16 日に内閣府が公表する 7～9 月期の実質 GDP（1 次速報値）は、前期比＋0.0%（前期比年
率換算＋0.1%）と予測する。設備投資や政府消費の増加に支えられ、かろうじて 4 四半期連続のプラス成長を
維持したものの、個人消費や輸出が前期の一時的な押し上げ要因の剥落もあって減少したことで、ほぼゼロ成長
にとどまったと見込まれる。なお、輸入が鉱物性燃料を中心に持ち直したことで外需寄与度は大きめのマイナスと
なるものの、公的在庫のプラス寄与により、おおむね相殺されたと考えられる。 
また、名目 GDP は、前期比＋0.9%（年率換算＋3.8%）となったと見込まれる。コスト増加分の価格転嫁が進
む中で、GDP デフレーターは前年比＋2.7%（季節調整済前期比では＋0.9%）と高めの伸びが続いた。 
今回、7～9 月期の実質 GDP はほぼゼロ成長にとどまる見込みだが、イラン情勢の悪化という逆風を踏まえれ
ば、国内景気は底堅さを維持している。先行きについては、輸入コストの高止まりに伴う価格転嫁の進展が景気
の重しとなる可能性はあるものの、良好な雇用・所得環境や企業業績の改善を背景に内需の回復基調が維持さ
れることなどから、国内景気は緩やかな回復を続けると期待される。

（注）内需寄与度、民間在庫、外需寄与度は実質 GDP に対する寄与度 
（出所）内閣府「四半期別 GDP 速報」により作成

### Page 2

2 / 2

需要項目別の動向は以下の通りである。

① 実質個人消費（前期比－0.1%） 
 良好な所得環境が維持される中、消費者マインドも持ち直しへ向かったものの、前期に耐久財
が自動車やエアコンを中心に一時的な要因によって増加した反動が生じたとみられる。価格転
嫁の進展による物価上昇、地震や台風といった自然災害の発生も重しとなった可能性がある。 
② 実質住宅投資（前期比－0.3%） 
 住宅投資に先行する住宅着工戸数が横ばいとなる中、建築コストが上昇していることもあり、住
宅投資は小幅ながら減少が続いたとみられる。 
③ 実質設備投資（前期比＋0.9%） 
 企業の投資意欲は省力化やデジタル需要などに支えられて堅調であり、設備投資は 3 四半期
ぶりに増加したと見込まれる。機械設備投資の供給側統計である資本財総供給（除く輸送機械）
も 7～9 月期は前期比＋5.3%と増加した。 
④ 実質民間在庫（前期比寄与度－0.0%ポイント） 
 前期に供給制約懸念の高まりを受けて、メーカーが製品在庫などの積み増しを進めた反動もあ
り、7～9 月期はマイナス寄与になったと考えられる。 
 なお、公的在庫については、政府による代替調達の進展により石油備蓄の減少ペースが大きく鈍
化したことから、前期比寄与度＋0.5%ポイントとプラス寄与になったとみられる。 
⑤ 実質政府最終消費支出（前期比＋0.3%） 
 高齢化が進む中、医療費・介護費を中心に底堅く推移したと予測する。 
⑥ 実質公共投資（前期比－2.7%） 
 建築・土木コストの上昇が続いている中、7～8 月の建設総合統計（出来高ベース：公共）は弱
めの動きとなったことから、実質公共投資は 2 四半期連続で減少したと見込まれる。 
⑦ 外需（前期比寄与度－0.5%ポイント、実質輸出：前期比－0.5%、実質輸入：同＋1.7%） 
 輸出は前期比－0.5%と減少したと予測する。財は堅調な米国向けや中東向けの持ち直しを受
けて増加した一方、サービスは前期に研究開発サービスが一時的な要因により上振れた反動
で減少したとみられる。 
 輸入は前期比＋1.7%と増加したと予測する。特に財が鉱物性燃料を中心に持ち直した。ただ
し、サービスについては減少した可能性がある。 
 結果、輸出から輸入を差し引いた外需の寄与度は－0.5%ポイントとマイナスになったと見込ま
れる。

公司簡介 
志聖成立於 1966 年，目前資本額 16.2 億
新台幣，為 G2C 集團當中的核心成員之一，
以紫外光、烘烤、壓膜塗佈、電漿製程等技
術提供相關壓膜、貼、撕膜、烘烤、電鍍相
關設備。公司初期以 PCB 設備起家，而後
伴隨台灣電子產業升級，切入半導體、組裝、
面板、太陽能等產業，近年陸續在 IC 載板、
高階 HDI、先進封裝、HBM 等高附加價值
市場發光發熱。1-3Q26 營收以產業別劃
分，半導體佔約 45%、先進 PCB 28%、傳
統 PCB 21%、其他電子 4%、FPD 3%。

主要客戶：台積電、日月光、臻鼎、力成、
Amkor、Micron、欣興、勝宏等 
主要競爭對手：EVG、TOK、SUSS、Yield 
Engineering Systems(YES)等

王彥鈞 stanley.wang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：座談會

志聖 (2467 TT) 
能見度高，獲利待驗證

永豐觀點 
Show me the margin。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進，目標價降至 358 元：(1) 3Q26 獲利表現不如預期，主因 PCB 比重拉升
及面對中國在地價格競爭，(2) 能見度高，但隨企業 AI Server 帶動 HDI/HLC PCB 
與 ABF 載板 2H26-2027 年接力放量，短期獲利改善有限。我們預估 2026/ 2027
年 EPS 為 14.01/19.92 元，目標 PE 則由 30x 下修至 18x，反映市場對設備類股評
價的調整，目標價隨之下修到 358 元(18x 2027 EPSF)。 
 近八季營收及 YoY 趨勢圖

### Page 2

志聖以光與熱為核心，發展熱處理平台：志聖成立於 1966 年，目前資本額 16.2 億
新台幣，為 G2C 集團當中的核心成員之一，以紫外光、烘烤、壓膜塗佈、電漿製
程等技術提供相關壓膜、貼、撕膜、烘烤、電鍍相關設備。公司初期以 PCB 設備
起家，而後伴隨台灣電子產業升級，切入半導體、組裝、面板、太陽能等產業，近
年陸續在 IC 載板、高階 HDI、先進封裝、HBM 等高附加價值市場發光發熱。公司
也與均豪、均華、東捷組成 G2C+聯盟，共同實現台灣先進封裝在地化。志聖主要
生產據點位於台灣林口、台中、中國廣州、昆山，並在台南、廣東、泰國、美國鳳
凰城有服務據點。1-3Q26 營收以產業別劃分，半導體佔約 45%、先進 PCB 28%、
傳統 PCB 21%、其他電子 4%、FPD 3%。主要競爭對手包含 EVG、TOK、SUSS、
Yield Engineering Systems(YES)等。客戶包含台積電、日月光、臻鼎、力成、
Amkor、Micron、欣興、勝宏等電子中上游廠商。 
3Q26 獲利不如預期：志聖 3Q26 營收 29.8 億(+7.0%QoQ，+93.9%YoY)，毛利
率 43.3%，稅後淨利 5.5 億(-13.4%QoQ，+162.1%YoY)，EPS 3.51 元。毛利率
表現不如預期主因出貨較多中國 PCB 用低階設備，使產品組合轉差，即半導體設
備/PCB 設備由 1H26 的 46%/46%變動至 1-3Q26 的 45%/49%，營業費用亦隨
獎酬增加及研發專案數的提升而高於預期。 
能見度高，但我們對獲利趨於審慎：管理層預期 2026 年底在手訂單約 100-110
億，較去年同期約 43 億元倍增，其中半導體佔比約 53%以上(以 OSAT 能見度較
高)、高階 PCB 35-37%。值得注意的是，半導體設備交期已由 4-10 個月拉長至 4-
13 個月；PCB 設備交期則約 3-8 個月，主因 OSAT 在廠房未完工前即下單，以及
上游零組件(機械手臂)交期的拉長所致，志聖持續以 G2C+聯盟和日系供應商溝通
並聯合採購，目前風險可控。展望後市，我們預期在晶圓廠先進封裝產能建置外溢
的環境下，志聖將透過挑單生產，降低傳統 PCB 接單比例以維持獲利率穩健成長。 
載板挖槽嵌入製程，2028 年再添動能：隨 AI 晶片尺寸持續放大，台系載板廠正
發展新的生產方式，以降低整片翹曲同時達到微縮線寬/線距的目的。志聖在此製
程中提供背面貼膜，以及 ABF 絕緣膜的壓合與撕膜設備。由於層數倍增將使壓合
與撕膜次數同步增加，加上載具尺寸放大推升單機價值，我們認為這將是志聖 
2028 年後載板設備的重要成長來源。 
財務預估：研究部預估志聖 2026 年營收 111.1 億元(+82.2%YoY)，毛利率 46.3%，
稅後淨利 22.7 億元(+173.0%YoY)，EPS 14.01 元(原估 16.94 元，下修主因本業
獲利表現不如預期及股本膨脹)。展望 2027 年，公司尚未給予明確展望，我們考量
短期 PCB/HLC 急單湧現壓抑毛利率，故下修獲利預估，預期 2027 年營收 148.4
億元(+33.5%YoY)，毛利率 46.9%，稅後淨利 32.2 億元(+42.2%YoY)，EPS 19.92
元(原估 25.15 元，下修主因獲利預估調降及股本膨脹)。

ab 17 November 2026
Global Research
ASPEED Technology Inc
Near-term BMC momentum peaking;
downgrade to Neutral
Three reasons for our downgrade to Neutral
We remain constructive about Aspeed’s growth potential and competitive positioning
longer term, but are downgrading the stock from Buy to Neutral on limited upside vs
expectation in 2027-28. Key considerations: 1) Aspeed’s BMC unit growth is highly tied
to overall Cloud AI TAM expansion, which is at rising risk especially for 2028 after the US

mid-terms and constraint of power / datacenter construction; 2) our analysis shows that
Aspeed may have over-shipped vs end market server units in 2026, leading to
uncertainty of order normalization in 2027 if tight supply eases / demand slows; 3) we
see limited shipment upside in next several quarters from high base in Q426.
Possible order moderation in 2027, after some over-build this year
Aspeed's BMC units grew 78% YoY to reach ~34mn in 2026, of which we estimate

9.5mn units for accelerator servers, and 23.5mn units for general purpose servers. Our
analysis indicates that Aspeed's BMC shipment for general purpose server likely exceeds
the end market server units at ~22.7m this year, implying excess inventory build at
hyperscalers which could cap the upside into 2027. We note that healthy shipment ratio
of Aspeed's BMC units vs end market should be ~80-90% considering Aspeed's 80%
market share, with 5-10% upside for switch, power supply etc. The ratio likely rises to

104% in 2026 from 70%+ in 2024-25. See Figure 1-3 for our analysis.   We lower 2027
BMC units from 48.2mn to 42.4mn, assuming the BMC shipment to reach near term
peak of 11-12m/Q in Q426-Q127, and to soften moderately to ~10m/Q in H227. We
still consider robust growth of AI accelerater BMCs at 82% in 2027E vs 76% in 2026E.
Product roadmap reinforces long-term leadership
Longer term, we remain constructive on Aspeed’s strong execution, accelerated product

roadmap, and rising silicon content per server. Aspeed remains highly focused on BMC
development, unlike  many competitors where BMC is one of several priorities. Its
roadmap, including more advanced BMCs such as the 6nm AST2800, enhanced security
features, and adjacent products such as SMC, I/O expanders and FPGA integration,
should reinforce its leading position in the Cloud AI market over the next 3-5 years. See
our view on Marvell’s infrastructure management controller (IMC) introduction.

Valuation: D/G to Neutral from Buy; lower target to NT$10,000 from NT$19,000
We trim 2027/28E EPS by 14%/11%. Our new estimates are 10/12% below VA
consensus. Aspeed's order outlook remains robust for 2027, but we see risk of
moderation in coming quarters. Downgrade to Neutral from Buy. Our new target of NT
$10,000 is based on 30x 2027E PE, as we roll back from 43x 2027-28E PE given the
rising uncertainty. 30x PE is below Aspeed's historical mid-cycle of 36x, but we view

reasonable considering the potential demand slowdown and 30% LT earnings CAGR.

ASPEED Technology Inc UBS Research
Thesis MapUBS Research THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Can BMC units continue to grow in the next two years after rising 78% in 2026E?
Although we remain constructive about Aspeed's long term execution and technology leadership,
we see a possible order moderation into 2027 after over-build in 2026E. We estimate that BMC
shipment will rise 78% YoY in 2026E and reach around 34m total BMC shipment units with 23.5m

dedicated for general purpose servers—this suggests potential pre-building of BMC inventory by
hyperscalers, as end market general purpose server units are only at 22.7m units in 2026E. In
addition, we are also monitoring the risk of AI data center buildout slowdown in 2028 post the recent
US elections and power constraints. We now forecast BMC shipment growth to moderate in
2027E/28E, with 25%/13% YoY unit shipment growth respectively.
Q: Can Aspeed maintain its dominant 70%-plus market share in server BMCs?

Yes. We expect Aspeed to maintain its market share in the next 3-4 years due to 1) We expect
migration to AST2700 to accelerate in 2027 from a mild 2026 ramp up, with new Intel and AMD CPU
platforms as well as key cloud ASIC projects and Nvidia's new Rubin Ultra AI GPU platform in 2027; 2)
Aspeed has enhanced its BMC offering by embedding flexibility to satisfy customer requirements; 3)
Aspeed is expanding its product portfolio to I/O expanders that integrate functionalities built on

FPGA, RoT security chips, and mini-BMCs (SMCs). 4) Aggressive business strategy to build inventory
for hyperscalers since 2025, which we believe should limit opportunity for competitors.
Q: Can Aspeed maintain overall gross margins at a high-60s level?
Yes. We expect Aspeed's GM to remain in the high-60s over the next few years. Drivers include: 1) the
roll-out of the higher-end AST2700 series, which could solidify Aspeed's competitive position and

help it sustain market share; 2) Aspeed managing back-end logistics for AST2700, reducing reliance
on GUC; and 3) Aspeed raising prices (effective Dec 1) for BMCs to pass through cost inflation.
We are constructive for Aspeed's long-term competitive positioning but are downgrading it to
Neutral. Our key considerations: 1) Aspeed’s BMC unit growth is highly tied to overall Cloud AI TAM
expansion, which is at rising risk especially for 2028 after the US mid-terms and constraint of power /

datacenter construction; 2) our analysis shows that Aspeed may have over-shipped vs end market
server units in 2026, leading to uncertainty of order normalization in 2027 if tight supply eases /
demand slows; 3) we see limited shipment upside in next several quarters from high base in Q426.
1) Our analysis indicates that Aspeed's BMC shipment for general purpose server likely exceeds the
end market server units at ~22.7m this year, implying excess inventory build at hyperscalers which

could cap the upside into 2027. 2) The outcome of recent US mid-terms creates higher uncertainty
for new AI data center build-out in 2028.
Aspeed's share price has rallied ~65% YTD (vs Taipei Exchange Index's 11% gain), but declined 54%
from its peak in Sep. The stock is now trading at 29x/22x 2027/28E PE, de-rated from its peak of
around 63x/48x in Sept, suggesting the market is taking a more conservative stance regarding overall
Cloud AI outlook and Aspeed's growth.

Company Description
ASPEED is the world's largest baseboard management controller (BMC) chipset provider.
ASPEED introduced the first generation of server management processors in 2005. It acquired
Emulex Pilot's remote server management chip business in 2016. Besides server management

chipsets, ASPEED also produces PC/AV extension solutions and image processing system-on-
chips (SoCs). In 2023, server management chipsets accounted for 88% of total sales.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We derive our price target for Aspeed from PE valuation methodology relative to its historical
trading range.
We believe key upside risks for Aspeed include: 1) stronger-than-expected server demand,
particularly from hyperscalers and from AI servers with less crowding out of traditional

servers; 2) further market share expansion; 3) faster-than-expected server platform upgrades;
4) faster-than-expected progress in expanding the addressable content within servers.
We believe key downside risks for Aspeed include: 1) weaker-than-expected server demand,
particularly from hyperscalers; 2) market share losses or more intense industry competition; 3)
slower-than-expected server platform upgrades; 4) slower-than-expected progress in
expanding the addressable content within servers.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over the next 12
months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is the one-year local
market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR) Stocks may be flagged
by the analyst to indicate that the stock's price target and/or rating may change in the near term, usually in response to an event that

may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have a 12-month investment horizon.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

### Page 3

Hon Precision (7769.TW)
25 November 2026 Citi Research
3
Hon Precision
Valuation
Our target price of NT$10,000 is based on a target PER multiple of 31x applied to the average of our 2027E/28E EPS estimates.
Our PER target of 31x is set at 0.5-std above its 1-year PER average, justified by its high share of the AI/HPC testing handler
market, which we estimate could support an earnings CAGR of >75% in 2025-28, higher than its 3-year CAGR at c40% in

2022-25. Our estimated >75% earning CAGR is also higher than the +40% average (Bloomberg consensus) of its global testing
equipment peers. As its peers have been trading at  average  35x PER on 2027/28 consensus EPS in the past 1 year, we believe
Hon Precision deserves a further re-rating to our target PER.
Risks
Our quantitative risk rating system, which looks at historical share price volatility, rates Hon Precision High Risk. However, we
are not assigning a High Risk rating as we forecast an earnings CAGR of >75%, significantly stronger than the growth outlook

for its global peers. Also, we note its dominant position in AI/HPC handler market and expanding market TAM opportunity,
which support our constructive view over its growth outlook.
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from reaching our target price: 1) Global AI demand slowdown  driving weaker
capacity build for semiconductor equipment; 2) unexpected share losses at its major AI projects; 3) slower-than-expected
capacity ramp at its new facilities, or the company taking a long time to find/secure new sites; 4) slower-than-expected

development of its CPO business; 5) shorter-than-expected testing time leading to lower demand for its products; 6) weak
adoption of high-premium cold plate products; and 7) increasing competition from new entrants.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do

Document 192: 410亿美元：制药业近三年最大并购的风向

Source PDF: all report pdf/410亿美元：制药业近三年最大并购的风向.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-25
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

赛诺菲以 410 亿美元收购 Insmed ，获得两款商业化药物及 III 期管线 TPIP ，成为辉瑞收购
Seagen 以来制药⾏业最⼤规模并购。交易背后，是 Dupixent 即将⾯临专利悬崖，赛诺菲亟
需寻找增⻓接⼒棒。这也释放出⾏业信号：⼤型药企正以⾼溢价换取商业化资产， Biotech
的估值逻辑正从管线潜⼒转向现⾦流确定性。
摘要：
11 ⽉ 24 ⽇，赛诺菲宣布以每股 175 美元的全现⾦对价收购美国⽣物制药公司 Insmed ，总对价 410
亿美元（约 376 亿欧元）。这是⾃ 2023 年 12 ⽉辉瑞以 430 亿美元收购 Seagen 之后，全球制药⾏业
规模最⼤的⼀笔并购。
市场对这个消息并不意外。收购传闻流出后， Insmed 股价已经被推⾼了⼀轮；消息正式落地当
天， Insmed 盘前再涨约 14% ，赛诺菲股价上涨近 6% 。买⽅和卖⽅的股东同时投出赞成票，这在
⼤型并购⾥不算常⻅。
当我们把交易条款、资产清单和赛诺菲⾃⼰的财务数据放在⼀起看，这笔并购的轮廓便慢慢清

晰起来。
三块资产
赛诺菲买到⼿的东西⼤致分三个部分：
图：赛诺菲收购 Insmed 获得的三块资产。来源：赛诺菲新闻稿、 Insmed
第⼀块是 Brinsupri （ brensocatib ）。
这是⼀款每⽇⼀次的⼝服 DPP1 抑制剂， 2025 年 8 ⽉在美国上市，⽤于⾮囊性纤维化⽀⽓管扩张
症（ NCFB ），⽬前已在美国、欧盟、英国和⽇本获批。它是全球⾸个获批的 DPP1 抑制剂，也是
⾸个且唯⼀获批⽤于 NCFB 的治疗药物，在这个适应症⾥，它没有对⼿。
2025 年 8 ⽉上市到 2026 年 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇，累计营收 10.9 亿美元，上市⼀年多就跨过重磅炸弹⻔槛。
Insmed ⾸席执⾏官 Will Lewis 的说法是， Brinsupri 在上市⼀年多时间⾥已经成为 NCFB 患者的标
准治疗。
第⼆块是 Arikayce 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

阿⽶卡星脂质体吸⼊混悬液，联合多药⽅案治疗难治性⻦分枝杆菌复合体（ MAC ）肺病，是
美、欧、⽇唯⼀获批的针对治疗难治⼈群的吸⼊疗法。
2025 年全年营收 4.338 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 19% ；今年 9 ⽉， FDA ⼜以优先审评受理了它的⼀项新
适应症申请。这款药的定位是慢病化的⼩体量资产：增速稳定，天花板不⾼，但现⾦流可⻅。
第三块是 III 期管线 TPIP 。
曲前列尼尔棕榈酸酯吸⼊粉雾剂，每⽇⼀次吸⼊的前列腺素类在研药物，针对肺动脉⾼压和纤
维化肺病等多种罕⻅呼吸适应症。这是交易⾥⻛险最⾼的部分，也是赛诺菲愿意为 “ 中期增⻓ ” ⽀
付对价的原因之⼀。
交易结构上， 410 亿美元全现⾦，由⾃有资⾦加债务融资完成，摩根⼤通与法国巴黎银⾏提供了
350 亿美元的过渡贷款。
溢价从何⽽来
按消息传出前的股价计算，赛诺菲⽀付了约 46% 的溢价（较上⼀交易⽇收盘价的溢价约 17.4% ，
传闻阶段的抢跑推⾼了基数）。
呼吸罕⻅病是⼀个特殊的赛道。 NCFB 和难治性 MAC 肺病都是慢性、进⾏性疾病，患者⼀旦开始

治疗，⽤药周期以年计，停药和换药的空间很⼩，患者粘性很强。Brinsupri 作为该适应症全球
唯⼀的获批药物，竞争格局在可预⻅的窗⼝内没有直接对⼿。
这类 “ 孤⼉药加慢病 ” 的组合，对应的正是资本市场最愿意给定价的属性：现⾦流可⻅度⾼。
Arikayce 连续 19% 的增速和 Brinsupri ⼀年⼗亿美元的放量曲线，都是这种可⻅度的注脚。
需要说明的是，这类资产同时也意味着患者池有天花板。 NCFB 的患病⼈群规模有限， Brinsupri
的增⻓依赖渗透率爬坡和地区扩展，⽽⾮患者池扩张。 Insmed 管理层给出的⼝径是，商业化产
品加 III 期管线合计具有 “ 数⼗亿美元级的峰值销售潜⼒ ” 。
专利悬崖的压迫
赛诺菲为什么此刻出⼿，答案在 Dupixent 的财务报表⾥。
2026 年第三季度， Dupixent 单季销售额 57 亿欧元，占公司当季净销售额接近四成； 2025 年全
年，这款药销售超过 150 亿欧元。⼀款药物撑起⼀家欧洲最⼤药企的近半收⼊，这种集中度在⼤

型 MNC ⾥⼏乎没有第⼆例。⽽它的美国专利预计在 2031 年前后到期。

### Page 3

图： Dupixent 占赛诺菲净销售额的⽐重。来源：赛诺菲 2026 年三季报
专利到期意味着什么，⾏业已经反复演⽰过：仿制药进⼊，价格体系坍塌，收⼊在两三年内滑
掉⼤部分。留给赛诺菲的时间窗，是从现在到 2031 年之前，⼤约三到五年。药物从临床到上市
需要⼗年上下的周期，如果靠⾃研，时间上已经来不及。并购是唯⼀能把 “ 接棒资产 ” 提前锁进时
间表的⽅式。
这样的基本事实决定了交易的设计逻辑。 Brinsupri 已经在放量， Arikayce 提供稳定现⾦流，两块
资产现在就能并⼊损益表； TPIP 的 III 期读出⼤约落在 2027—2028 年，正好接在交割之后。
如果⼀切顺利，到 2031 年 Dupixent 仿制药进场时，赛诺菲⼿⾥已经有⼀组年销售数⼗亿美元的
呼吸资产在运转。
⻛向： M&A 补充管线的周期
把镜头拉远，这笔交易透露的⻛向⽐交易本⾝更值得关注。
图： 2023 年以来制药业⼤额并购序列。来源： Reuters 、公司公告
辉瑞 /Seagen （ 430 亿美元， 2023 年 12 ⽉）之后，⾏业整整三年没有出现同量级的并购。这三年

⾥， MNC 的策略重⼼先后放在减肥药竞赛、内部管线重整和早期 BD ⼩额交易上。
赛诺菲 /Insmed 打破了沉寂，⽽且打破的⽅式带有明确的信号意义：买⽅已经退⽽求其次，不再
执着增⻓故事，更重要的是买到确定性—— 已经上市的药、已经跑出来的销售曲线、已经获批
的适应症。
这个选择的 “ 对照组 ” 是⾃研。⼀款新药从⽴项到上市平均需要⼗年左右，临床失败率超过九成；
⽽买⼀个已经商业化的资产组合，付出 40%—50% 的溢价，可以把失败⻛险和周期⻛险⼀并转
移掉。
在专利悬崖集中到来、投资者对 “ 专利到期后还有没有增⻓ ” 的追问越来越紧的环境⾥， MNC ⽤溢
价换确定性的意愿系统性上升。赛诺菲的 350 亿美元过渡贷款也说明，融资环境给了他们这样做
的空间，利率环境宽松，资本市场对⾼评级药企的⼤额并购融资保持开放。
值得留意的是这个⻛向的传导路径。当 MNC 开始为 “ 已商业化的呼吸罕⻅病资产 ” ⽀付 410 亿美
元，它同时给整个市场的同类资产定了价：有患者粘性、竞争格局⼲净、现⾦流可⻅的细分领

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

对研发型企业⽽⾔，这意味着⼀个被并购的窗⼝正在打开；对投资⽅⽽⾔，这意味着资产筛选

勁，毛利率 34.64% (QoQ-0.98 ppt)，主因第三季認列部分 2025 
年承接之較低毛利訂單，微幅稀釋單季毛利表現。受惠於營收

益  0.16 億元(QoQ-11.71%），主要歸因於利息收入增加及外匯

市場受惠於成熟製程晶圓廠建置與電子特化在地化需求，營收
佔比躍升至 48.20%，成為單季最大營收來源區域；台灣市場維
持  47.40% 的高檔佔比，反映本土先進製程晶圓大廠擴產動能

洲市場與東南亞分別佔 2.20% 與  1.60% 。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG：  
上品制定多項 ESG 目標，並逐年強化執行。2023 年，公司透過智能化水資
源管理系統，節省超過 27,300 噸的水，並在節能方面減少 184 萬度的電力
消耗。公司同時推動自發自用的太陽能發電計劃，預計 2024 年將建設 252kW
的太陽能系統，並在 2025 年進一步擴大其太陽能自發電的使用比例。 
公司簡介：  
上品專注於鐵氟龍內存設備的生產與加工，產品線涵蓋鐵氟龍內襯設備、管
件及應用材料等，累積長達 45 年氟素樹脂(鐵氟龍)材料改性與內襯設備製
造經驗，於 12/2021 正式掛牌上市。 
在產業鏈位置上，公司上游主要向國際氟化學大廠科慕 (Chemours) 與大金
(Daikin) 採購 PTFE 、PFA、FEP 等原廠樹脂原料；中游則由上品完成兩階
段核心製造，先依不同氟素物性加工製成板材、管材、棒材及射出半成品，
再依據客戶廠區耐壓、耐溫及結構需求客製設計金屬設備外觀，最後以專利
內襯工法將氟材料緊密貼合於金屬內層。 
在半導體曝光微影、薄膜沉積、化學機械研磨 (CMP) 、濕式清洗與蝕刻製
程中，液態電子化學品有 88% 屬於強腐蝕性酸鹼液體，包括硫酸
(H2SO4)、氫氟酸 (HF) 、鹽酸 (HCl) 、硝酸 (HNO3) 、磷酸 (H3PO4) 、氨水
(NH4OH) 與雙氧水 (H2O2) ，另有 12% 為光阻液 (PR) 、光阻稀釋液
(PGMEA/PGME) 與顯影液 (TMAH) 等有機溶劑。上品透過 TEFPASS® 
專利氟素塗層與內襯技術，確保上述高腐蝕化學品在儲運過程中達到極致
潔淨與近乎零金屬離子析出的嚴苛標準，直接保障先進製程晶圓良率，構築
極高的客戶認證門檻。 
3Q26 成長來自半導體產業需求，已成絕對核心成長引擎： 
3Q26 營收 15.98 億元 (QoQ+22.48%) ，較  2Q26 的  13.05 億元顯著放量，
受惠於半導體晶圓廠建廠與擴產需求強勁，毛利率 34.64% (QoQ-0.98 ppt)，
主因第三季認列部分 2025 年承接之較低毛利訂單，微幅稀釋單季毛利表
現。 
在費用與營業槓桿方面，3Q26 費用為 1.54 億元 (QoQ-6.87%) ，受惠於營
收規模大幅擴張，單季營業費用率顯著收斂至 9.63% (QoQ-3.04 ppt) ，進而
推動營業利益達 4.00 億元 (QoQ+33.43%) ，營益率逆勢擴張至 25.02% 
(QoQ+2.05 ppt)。  
業外與稅後獲利部分，3Q26 業外淨收益 0.16 億元(QoQ-11.71%），主要歸
因於利息收入增加及外匯匯兌變動。稅後淨利 3.41 億元，稅後 EPS4.27 元。
各業務部門、產品線及地區別營運表現： 
3Q26 半導體應用營收佔比 95.10% 呈現逐季攀升態勢；石化化工產業佔比
縮減至 3.10%；面板與太陽能產業佔比為 1.40% ，其他產業佔 0.40% 。  
內襯設備 3Q26 佔比 69.70%，涵蓋半導體大宗化學品儲槽、反應槽及運送
槽車；其他材料佔比 16.50% ，包含 PTFE 與  PFA 氟素半成品板材、管材
及精密射出件；內襯管件 (Lined Pipe & Fitting) 受惠於晶圓廠進入無塵室

3 群益投顧 
長距離化學品輸送管網鋪設階段，佔比擴張至 10.20%；工程服務因現場裝
配認列進度隨工期波動，佔比回落至 3.70%。  
中國與台灣兩大市場合計貢獻 3Q26 營收 95.60% 。其中中國市場受惠於
成熟製程晶圓廠建置與電子特化在地化需求，營收佔比躍升至 48.20%，成
為單季最大營收來源區域；台灣市場維持 47.40% 的高檔佔比，反映本土
先進製程晶圓大廠擴產動能穩固。美國市場因亞利桑那州新廠首期工程認
列告一段落、後續期程尚處規劃與等待階段，3Q26 營收佔比滑落至 0.50%；
歐洲市場與東南亞分別佔 2.20% 與  1.60% 。  
投資建議：半導體應用佔比逐季擴張至 95.10%，晶圓廠擴產帶動營收增長；
氟素樹脂改性與 TEFPASS® 專利內襯工藝，並延伸至晶圓廠廢酸回收純化
系統；目前營收主要來自台灣、中國，美國市場建廠認列暫入空窗期。預估
2027 稅後 EPS15.34 元，投資建議 Trading Buy，目標價 296 元。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 1

(034020) 
두산에너빌리티 
2026.10.30

에너지/인프라/배터리 황성현, Ph.D. 
6878 / tjdgus2009@eugenefn.com

펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한 일시적 주가 하락 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 80,000 원(하향) 
 현재주가 56,700 원(10/29)

 [투자의견] 당사는 두산에너빌리티에 대해 목표주가를 80,000원(2027년 
BPS의 6.2배)으로 27% 하향하며, 투자의견 BUY를 유지한다. 실적은 견조
한 회복을 보이고 있으나, 최근 GE Vernova(GEV) 등 글로벌 AI 및 에너지 
밸류체인 Peer 주가 조정을 반영해 멀티플을 하향 조정했다. 체코 시공 계
약 등 일부 노이즈에 의해 주가가 급락한 만큼 매수 기회라고 판단한다.  
 [3Q26 Review] 매출액 4.4조원(-5.8%qoq, +14.7%yoy), 영업이익 
2,612억원(-16.9%qoq, +90.6%yoy)을 기록해 당사 추정에 부합하는 무
난한 실적을 기록했다. 에너빌리티 부문은 매출액 1.8조원, 영업이익 1,211
억원(영업이익률 6.6%, +2.3%p qoq)을 기록하며 원자력·가스터빈 중심의 
고수익 사업 믹스 개선으로 분기 수익성이 재차 개선됐다. 당기순이익은 
149억원(-93.4%qoq)에 그쳤으나, 원달러 환율 급락으로 발생한 환손실 
반영 때문으로 이익 체력과는 무관하다고 판단한다. 3Q26 미국 빅테크 데
이터센터향 가스터빈 첫 출하를 달성했으며, 수주잔고는 에너빌리티 부문 
기준 26.6조원(+70.9%yoy)으로 사상 최대치를 경신했다. 
 [4Q26 Preview] 매출액 4.8조원, 영업이익 2,640억원으로 유사한 실적 
흐름을 전망한다. 4Q 가스터빈 인도와 체코 두코바니 원전 진행률 반영으로 
에너빌리티 부문 외형 성장이 기대된다. 3Q26 별도 기준 영업이익률이 
8.5%까지 개선된 만큼 에너빌리티 부문의 분기 실적 개선 추세는 유효하다
고 판단한다. 연간 수주 목표 13.3조원 달성을 위한 잔여 수주(3.3조원 +α)
가 예정되어 있으며, 대형원전을 제외하더라도 가이던스 달성은 무난할 것
이라 판단한다. 세부적으로는 SMR 1조원(계약 조건 합의 완료 단계로 연내 
체결 가능), 신재생 7,000억원(해상풍력 기자재·EPC 2건 협의 완료 단계), 
해외 복합 EPC 5,000억원(국내 1건, 해외 2건)이 기대된다. 
 [가스터빈] 3Q26 누적 가스터빈 13기·스팀터빈 11기·LTSA 6건을 달성하
며 연간 10기 이상 수주·수행 역량이 안정적으로 유지되고 있다. 2030년 
슬롯 예약 물량의 본계약 전환은 2027년, 2031년 슬롯은 2028년으로 예
상되며, 수주잔고 증가가 기대된다. 가스터빈 수요 증가를 감안하면 추가 캐
파 증설 결정이 이르면 연내 이루어질 전망이다. 
 [원전] 10월 NuScale 1개 모듈 제작 계약을 체결했으며, PPA 지연 여부와 
무관하게 완결된 계약으로 실적에 반영될 전망이다. X-energy는 연내 기자
재 및 부품의 후속 계약을 추진 중이다. TerraPower 주기기 3종 중 2종 계
약이 완료되어 제작에 착수했으며, Rolls-Royce SMR·i-SMR 등 파트너십 
확대도 지속되고 있다. 이외에 대미투자 관련 기자재 발주는 미국 부지 확정 
및 한국 의회 승인 절차가 남아있어 내년 발주가 기대된다. 기존 웨스팅하우
스 AP1000 Vogtle 계약 대비 업무 범위 확대(원자로·증기발생기 외 추가 
품목) 가능성이 있어 긍정적으로 평가한다.

### Page 2

두산에너빌리티

2_ Eugene Research Center 
설명회 Q&A 
Q1. 4Q26 SMR 수주 목표 약 1 조원의 실현 가능성 
- 고객사와 GTC 등 주요 계약 조건에 대한 합의를 대부분 완료하고 계약 체결 대기 중 
- 연내 계약 가능성이 있어 수주 목표는 의미 있는 수준

Q2. NuScale 모듈 1 기 제작 계약의 PPA 지연 영향 
- 완결된 제작 계약으로 PPA 체결이 지연되더라도 해당 모듈의 제작 및 진행에는 문제없음

Q3. AP1000 장주기 기자재 선발주 후 인허가 지연 시 회계 처리 및 사업 영향 
- DOE·DOC 및 한국 정부 재원을 활용해 EPC 계약·착공 지연과 무관하게 기자재 제작 가능 
- 프로젝트 중단 시 웨스팅하우스의 다른 프로젝트로 기자재 전용 가능성 검토

Q4. 체코 원전 컨소시엄 변경 시 참여 및 사업 계획 
- 현지 지점 운영과 시공인력 상주로 시공 환경 자료 확보 
- 한수원과 초기 인허가·설계에 참여하고, 주기기 공급·설치 역량을 바탕으로 경쟁력 확보 기대

Q5. 체코 원전 EPC 사업의 수익성 확보 가능성 
- 한수원 가이드에 따라 수익성을 고려한 입찰 진행 예정

Q6. 웨스팅하우스 SPV 및 폴란드 AP1000 장주기 기자재 발주 진행 상황 
- DOE·DOC 프로젝트와 폴란드 AP1000 3 기 기자재 조기 발주 협의 중 
- 연내 수주 여부는 불확실하나 조기 수주 추진

Q7. 3Q26 영업이익률 개선 요인 
- 원자력·가스터빈 중심의 사업 믹스 개선이 수익성에 기여 
- 제작 진행률과 마일스톤에 따라 이익 인식

### Page 3

두산에너빌리티

Eugene Research Center _3 
Q8. 4Q26 복합화력 수주 목표 5,000 억원의 달성 가능성 
- 국내 1 건, 해외 2 건 협의 중이며 국내 프로젝트의 수주 가능성이 높은 상황 
- 대미 투자 물량은 기존 2031 년까지의 예약계약 납기와 비교해 검토 중

Q9. 3Q26 에너빌리티부문 당기순손실 원인 
- 급격한 환율 하락에 따른 약 800 억원의 환산손실이 주요 원인

Q10. 대미 투자 관련 기자재 발주 시점 
- 미국 부지 확정, 웨스팅하우스 사업제안 및 한국 정부 심사·국회 승인 절차 필요 
- 연말~내년 초 발주 여부는 불확실하나 조기 발주 추진

Q11. 대미 투자 프로젝트의 기자재 공급 범위 확대 가능성 
- 원자로·증기발생기 외 추가 품목까지 공급 범위 확대를 위해 웨스팅하우스와 협상 중

Q12. 장주기 기자재 선발주 후 인허가 지연 시 실적 영향 
- 기자재 출하 시 수금 조건이 충족되며 선발주 자금도 사전 확보 
- 보글·VC 서머 사례에서 기자재 대금을 회수해 프로젝트 지연에 따른 실적 영향은 제한적

Q13. 4Q26 신재생·풍력 수주 목표 약 7,000 억원의 달성 가능성 
- 해상풍력 기자재·EPC 계약 및 유지보수 계약 추진 중 
- 주요 계약 협의가 상당 부분 완료돼 일정 지연이 없다면 연내 목표 달성 전망

Q14. 중형 가스터빈 개발 및 해외 진출 계획 
- 대형 가스터빈은 한국·미국 중심으로 추진하며 중동 고객사와도 협의 중 
- 휴스턴 서비스 허브를 기반으로 미국 데이터센터 시장 대응 
- 중형 가스터빈은 시제품 제작 및 시험 진행 후 2028 년 하반기 출하 목표

### Page 4

두산에너빌리티

4_ Eugene Research Center 
Q15. NuScale 모듈 1 기 선발주 배경 
- 대규모 일괄 발주에 대비한 제작 경험 확보 및 납기 리스크 완화를 위해 사전 제작 추진

Q16. X-energy 연내 후속 계약 대상 품목 
- 증기발생기 전열관 및 주단 소재 등 장주기 품목의 사전 발주 추진

Q17. DOE 프로젝트의 장주기 기자재 공급 범위 확대 가능성 
- 웨스팅하우스 공급망 상황에 따라 추가 품목 협상 중 
- 과거 보글·VC 서머보다 넓은 공급 범위 기대

Q18. 웨스팅하우스 지분 투자와 기자재 계약 시점의 연계 가능성 
- 지분 투자는 내년, 기자재 계약은 연내 또는 내년 초 목표로 시점이 일치할지는 불확실

Q19. X-energy 장주기 기자재의 소재·부품 구분 
- 증기발생기 전열관은 부품, 주단 소재는 소재에 해당 
- 전열관은 공급 병목에 대비해 조기 발주하고 주단 소재는 설계 확정 후 제작 준비

Q20. 복합화력 주기기 수주의 기존·신규 고객 비중 
- 기존 고객과 추가 예약계약을 협의 중이며 신규 글로벌 고객과도 예약계약 협상 진행

Q21. 주요 SMR 사업 외 파트너십 확대 가능성 
- Rolls-Royce 사업 추진 및 GE Hitachi BWRX-300 주단 소재 예약계약 체결 
- i-SMR 은 2035 년 운전을 목표로 2028 년 이전 발주가 필요해 관련 협의 진행

Q22. X-energy 전열관·주단 소재 수주 물량 규모 
- 다우케미컬 초도 사업은 80MW 원자로 4 개 모듈로 구성되며, 해당 4 개 모듈분 부품·소재 공급

### Page 5

두산에너빌리티

Eugene Research Center _5 
Q23. 중동 지정학적 불안정에 따른 프로젝트·물류 영향 
- 기존 프로젝트의 운송비 증가분을 발주처·개발사와 협의 중 
- 신규 계약에는 비용 보상 및 공기 지연 관련 조항 반영

Q24. 가스터빈 생산능력 증설 규모 및 시기 
- 현재 생산능력 증설 중이며 2031 년 출하분까지 예약 접수 
- 시장 수요에 따라 추가 증설 검토

Q25. 2030 년 가스터빈 예약 물량의 실제 계약 전환 시점 
- 2030 년 출하 물량은 대부분 내년 실제 계약 여부 결정 전망 
- 2031 년 출하 물량은 2028 년경 계약 여부 결정 예상

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에 동 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

摘要：

退。
26 ⽇，美股感恩节休市。法国 CAC 40 指数涨幅扩⼤⾄ 2% ，⽇经 225 指数收盘上涨 0.9% ，⾸尔
综指收盘上涨 0.3% 。 MSCI 亚太指数上涨 1.5% ，有望录得 11 个交易⽇以来的第⼆个单⽇涨幅，
科技股表现居前。
个股⽅⾯，铠侠在东京市场⼤涨 13% ，此前该公司宣布与英伟达合作探索 GPU 内存容量扩展；
韩国芯⽚巨头亦同步⾛⾼。
本轮反弹承接了周三美股假期前的尾盘买盘。当天纳斯达克 100 指数在盘中⼀度较峰值下跌
20% 、触及熊市⻔槛后，收复了⼤部分跌幅。周四美国市场因感恩节休市，全球美债现货交易

暂停。此前⼀⽇纽约时段，美债 10 年期收益率下降 8 个基点⾄ 4.85% 。
震荡尚未平息  AI 信仰仍在重建
此次回升发⽣在经历惨淡 11 ⽉之后，释放出投资者愿意重返股市的积极信号。⾃美国中期选举
以来，市场剧烈震荡，英伟达的领导层危机加剧了动荡，抛售将主要股指推⼊回调区间。围绕 AI
建设成本与潜在回报的疑问依然存在。
“ 我们确实看到了⼀些反弹， ” ⾹港 HashKey Capital 合伙⼈ Mia Mai 表⽰， “ 但我们还不会称之为全
⾯复苏，主要因为对 AI 的重新信⼼尚未到来。⼈们仍在观望。 ”

韩国央⾏加息并上调 2027 年经济增⻓预测后，韩国 3 年期国债收益率⼩幅上⾏。

Document 291: AI服务器需求引爆戴尔业绩！营收翻倍、上调全年指引，1323亿美元订单积压创新高｜财报见闻

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI服务器需求引爆戴尔业绩！营收翻倍、上调全年指引，1323亿美元订单积压创新高｜财报见闻.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

戴尔 Q3 财季营收 549.37 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 103% ；⾮ GAAP 每股收益 9.45 美元，同⽐增⻓
265% ，两项指标均⼤幅超过市场预期。当季新增 AI 服务器订单 602 亿美元，期末积压订单
升⾄ 1323 亿美元。全年业绩指引三度上调，预计 2027 财年营收达 2020 亿美元，同⽐增⻓
78% ，预计 Q4 财季营收达 563 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 69% 。戴尔盘后股价波动不⼤，⽬前上涨
1.8% 。
摘要：
戴尔科技公布的最新财报显⽰， AI 基础设施投资热潮正以前所未有的速度转化为公司收⼊。第三
财季营收同⽐翻倍， AI 优化服务器收⼊增⻓近三倍，新订单和积压订单均创历史新⾼，推动公司
第三次上调本财年业绩指引。
当地时间 11 ⽉ 24 ⽇，戴尔公布截⾄ 2026 年 10 ⽉ 30 ⽇的 2027 财年第三季度业绩。财报显⽰，公司
当季营收达到 549.37 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 103% ；⾮ GAAP 每股收益为 9.45 美元，同⽐增⻓
265% ，两项指标均⼤幅超过市场预期。分析师此前平均预期营收为 493.2 亿美元、调整后每股

收益为 6.59 美元。
更重要的是，戴尔 AI 优化服务器业务继续加速增⻓。第三财季，该业务收⼊达到 220.31 亿美
元，同⽐增⻓ 291% ；公司当季新增 AI 服务器订单 602 亿美元，期末 AI 服务器积压订单达到 1323
亿美元，均创历史新⾼。
受此推动，戴尔将 2027 财年全年营收预期上调⾄ 2020 亿美元，并将 AI 优化服务器收⼊⽬标提⾼
⾄ 780 亿美元。
不过，戴尔的增⻓也呈现出明显的业务分化：基础设施业务成为业绩增⻓的核⼼引擎，⽽ PC 业
务增速明显放缓。
与此同时，内存芯⽚供应紧张、零部件成本上涨正在给服务器⼚商带来新的定价和利润率挑
战。如何在扩⼤ AI 服务器交付规模的同时维持盈利能⼒，将成为市场接下来关注的重点。
财报发布后，戴尔盘后股价波动不⼤，截⾄发稿，上涨 1.8% 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

AI 服务器业务成为增⻓核⼼，积压订单突破千亿美元
戴尔第三财季的增⻓⼏乎由基础设施业务驱动。
财报显⽰，基础设施解决⽅案集团（ ISG ）当季营收达到 419.74 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 198% ；营业
利润达到 71.18 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 308% ，营业利润率升⾄ 17.0% ，⾼于上年同期的 12.4% 。
其中， AI 优化服务器收⼊达到 220.31 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 291% ，成为最主要的增⻓来源。传统服
务器及⽹络业务收⼊也达到 150.13 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 235% ；存储业务收⼊为 49.30 亿美元，同
⽐增⻓ 24% 。
订单情况同样强劲。戴尔当季新增 AI 服务器订单达到 602 亿美元，期末积压订单升⾄ 1323 亿美
元。公司副董事⻓兼⾸席运营官 Jeff Clarke 表⽰，戴尔正在对积压订单进⾏严格筛选，评估客⼾
资质以及资⾦、⼟地和电⼒资源的可获得性。
Clarke 还表⽰，⽬前全球已有超过 7500 家客⼾部署戴尔 AI Factory 解决⽅案。通过在发货前完成

整机柜测试，并在交付后六⼩时内完成部署，戴尔希望缩短客⼾从设备交付到模型运⾏的时
间。
这⼀业务扩张也推动戴尔进⼀步调整⾃⾝业务结构。 Clarke 在财报声明中表⽰，基础设施解决⽅
案集团如今已经贡献了公司绝⼤部分利润和增⻓。
全年业绩指引第三次上调， AI 服务器收⼊⽬标提⾼⾄ 780 亿美元
强劲的季度业绩促使戴尔再次提⾼全年业绩预期。
公司预计， 2027 财年全年营收将达到 2020 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 78% ，⾼于此前 1920 亿美元的预
测。全年⾮ GAAP 每股收益预期从 25.50 美元上调⾄ 29.68 美元，同⽐增⻓ 188% 。
AI 优化服务器业务的全年收⼊⽬标也从 740 亿美元提⾼⾄ 780 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 216% 。
对于第四财季，戴尔预计营收将达到 563 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 69% ；⾮ GAAP 每股收益预计为 8.40
美元，同⽐增⻓ 116% 。

### Page 3

这⼀展望同样⾼于华尔街此前预期。根据路透社援引的 LSEG 数据，分析师此前预计戴尔 2027 财
年全年营收约为 1944.8 亿美元，
此外，戴尔董事会批准新增 250 亿美元股票回购授权。公司第三财季通过股票回购和股息向股东
返还了 49 亿美元，前三财季经营活动现⾦流达到 107.65 亿美元。
内存成本上涨，服务器⼚商开始向客⼾转嫁压⼒
在 AI 服务器需求快速增⻓的同时，供应链成本问题正在成为⾏业新的变量。
内存芯⽚价格上涨尤其值得关注。此前，戴尔在 2027 财年第⼆季度的监管⽂件中已经披露， AI
优化解决⽅案需求强劲，加上内存制造商产能受限，导致全球供应紧张以及内存组件成本⼤幅
上涨。公司还指出，部分组件采购成本正在上升。
11 ⽉ 22 ⽇，《⾦融时报》报道称，戴尔、惠普企业、联想和超微电脑等服务器⼚商正在要求部分
客⼾为已经签订合同的 AI 服务器⽀付更⾼价格，部分订单的涨价幅度达到 25% ⾄ 45% 。
报道援引知情⼈⼠称，部分 GPU 服务器价格上涨了 20% ⾄ 30% ，内存占成本较⾼的 CPU 服务器

节点涨幅甚⾄更⼤。内存价格上涨使服务器⼚商难以继续按照此前合同价格交付设备。
戴尔还因⼀笔服务器交易⾯临法律纠纷。英国交易公司 XTX Markets 在爱尔兰起诉戴尔，争议涉
及⼀笔价值 5.49 亿美元的服务器合同。 XTX ⽅⾯指称，戴尔要求在原合同⾦额之外额外⽀付
6200 万⾄ 7000 万美元。相关纠纷仍在进⾏中。
这意味着， AI 服务器市场虽然需求旺盛，但⼚商的盈利能⼒并不只取决于订单规模，还取决于其
能否将不断上涨的内存和其他零部件成本转嫁给客⼾。
值得注意的是，戴尔此次财报并未单独披露内存涨价对第三财季利润的具体影响，也没有给出
未来内存成本的量化预测。因此，不能仅凭营收和利润增⻓，就认定公司已经完全消化了内存
成本压⼒。
PC 业务增⻓放缓，消费端需求⾯临压⼒
与基础设施业务的强劲表现相⽐，戴尔传统 PC 业务增⻓相对疲弱。
财报显⽰，客⼾端解决⽅案集团（ CSG ）第三财季营收为 128.86 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 3% ；营业利
润为 7.04 亿美元，同⽐下降 6% ，营业利润率从上年同期的 6.0% 降⾄ 5.5% 。

其中，商业 PC 业务收⼊ 113 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 6% ；消费 PC 业务收⼊ 15.86 亿美元，同⽐下降
15% 。
这表明，企业客⼾设备更新需求仍提供⼀定⽀撑，但消费端表现相对疲弱。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

增⻓正在改变公司的收⼊和利润结构，但 PC 业务能否恢复增⻓，以及零部件成本上涨是否会侵

Document 292: AI炒股新玩法！盯上3000万散户，提前24小时预测交易行为

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI炒股新玩法！盯上3000万散户，提前24小时预测交易行为.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-25
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

以⾊列 AI 公司 42AI 通过分析近 3000 万名投资者的交易⾏为，预测散⼾交易动向，帮助券商
优化对冲策略。与此同时， AI 正从辅助散⼾构建交易策略，延伸⾄机构投研和⾃主投资。
AI 降低了投资⻔槛，却也放⼤了机构的数据与资源优势，散⼾想要获取超额收益或将更加
困难。
摘要：
谁能想到，在⾦融市场上， AI 最先研究透的可能不是市场，⽽是散⼾。
最近，以⾊列 AI 创业公司 42AI 正式结束隐⾝研发状态，并披露了 6250 万美元的种⼦轮融资。
这家公司的⽣意很有意思。过去⼏⼗年，华尔街投⼊了⼤量的⼈⼒和资⾦，试图预测⾦融资产
的涨跌。但 42AI 换了⼀个思路：既然⾏情这么难预测，能不能预测投资者的⾏为？
⽐如，⻩⾦突然⼤涨，哪些散⼾会忍不住追⾼，哪些散⼾会选择获利了结，⼜有多少⼈会加杠
杆？
42AI 做的，就是把这些⾏为变成可以量化、预测的数据，再将预测结果卖给券商，帮助券商制
定对冲策略。
⽬前， 42AI 已经与 10 家⼤型券商展开合作，涉及近 3000 万名投资者的客⼾数据。据其创始⼈透

露，通过优化对冲策略， 42AI 已经为⼀家券商创造了约 3000 万美元的额外价值。
有意思的是，当⼀批 AI 公司开始帮机构研究散⼾时，另⼀批 AI 公司正在帮助散⼾提升投资能⼒。
⽐如，今年 10 ⽉获得红杉资本投资的 Catalyst ，可以直接将普通投资者的想法转化为⾃动交易策
略。过去需要⼀个专业投资团队才能完成的策略开发、回测和⾃动交易，如今⼀个散⼾借助 AI 就
能完成。
听起来， AI 似乎正在拉平散⼾与专业投资机构之间的能⼒差距。但研究了⼀圈 AI 在⾦融交易领域
的应⽤后，硅基君发现，事情可能没有那么乐观。
预测不了⾏情，那就预测散⼾
预测⾏情，可能是⾦融市场上最吃⼒不讨好的事情之⼀。
今年 1 ⽉，⾼盛还在担⼼全球原油供应过剩，预计今年布伦特原油均价只有 56 美元 / 桶。结果美国
和伊朗打了起来，国际油价⼀度突破 100 美元 / 桶。
但如果换个思路呢？
虽然不知道明天油价是涨是跌，但如果油价突然上涨 10% ，投资者会做出什么反应，或许更容
易预测。毕竟，⾏情千变万化，但⼀个⼈的交易习惯、⻛险偏好往往有规律可循。

42AI 就是顺着这个思路做起了⽣意。它瞄准了⼀类特殊的⾦融机构 ——CFD （差价合约）交易
商。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

这类券商主要为投资者提供⻩⾦、原油、外汇等资产的杠杆交易服务，赚钱的⽅式主要有两
种。
⼀种是赚交易费⽤。⽐如，客⼾做多⻩⾦，券商就在外部市场建⽴等额的多头仓位，将价格波
动的⻛险对冲掉，赚取交易费⽤与对冲成本之间的差额。
另⼀种则是直接与客⼾做对⼿盘。如果券商选择不对冲，那么客⼾亏 100 美元，券商就赚 100 美
元，反之亦然（暂不考虑交易费⽤）。
现实中，这类券商往往只会对冲部分交易⻛险，以争取更⼤的盈利空间。因此，哪些⻛险可以
⾃⼰承担，哪些⻛险必须对冲，直接关系到券商能赚多少钱。
42AI 做的，就是帮券商把这笔账算得更准。
为此， 42AI ⼏乎将投资者与券商打交道的整个过程，都纳⼊了 AI 的研究范围。除了历史交易记
录、持仓、⼊⾦和出⾦记录外，就连客⼾给客服打了多少次电话、通话时的语⽓和情绪，都是 AI
的分析对象。
⽐如，有些投资者喜欢在亏损后不断加仓，有些投资者稍微赚点钱就急着卖出，还有些投资者
⼀遇到剧烈⾏情就忍不住频繁交易。
42AI 将这些⾏为转化为可量化的个⼈特征，再通过基于 Transformer 架构构建的⾏为世界模型，

预测投资者在不同市场环境下的反应。
据 42AI 披露，⽬前其系统最远可以提前 24 ⼩时预测客⼾的交易品种、⽅向和交易量。
这些信息对券商有什么价值？假设⼀家券商的客⼾持有 100 份⻩⾦多单、 80 份空单，多空相互抵
销后，券商只需要在外部市场建⽴ 20 份多单，就能对冲掉剩余的⻛险。
但如果接下来有 40 份多单被平仓，客⼾的整体持仓就会从净多头变成净空头。如果券商没能及
时调整仓位，原本⽤于降低⻛险的对冲交易，反⽽可能放⼤亏损。
42AI 的价值，就是让券商在客⼾实际下单之前，就能对这些变化有所准备。
尤其是在极端⾏情下，这种能⼒相当值钱。
2025 年 10 ⽉ 21 ⽇，国际⾦价单⽇下跌约 5% 。据 42AI 创始⼈ Amit Gal-Or 透露，⼀家经营数⼗年
的⼤型 CFD 交易商，在这次⾏情中亏掉了 5000 万美元。
两个⽉后，这家券商找到了 42AI 。双⽅利⽤历史数据重新模拟了当天的交易情况，发现如果当
时采⽤这套系统，券商有望少亏数千万美元。
这笔账算完，双⽅很快便达成了合作。

⽬前， 42AI 除了向券商收取固定费⽤，还可以按照为券商创造的价值进⾏分成。
AI 正在全⾯介⼊⾦融交易
42AI 只是 AI 进⼊⾦融交易领域的⼀个缩影。
过去两年， AI 在⾦融⾏业的应⽤已经开始从信息搜集、辅助投研，逐渐向投资决策和交易执⾏延
伸。据硅基观察不完全统计，⽬前全球已有 12 家代表性 AI 创业公司布局这⼀领域。

### Page 3

从商业模式来看，⼤致可以分为三类。
第⼀类，是帮助普通投资者构建交易策略。
⽐如，今年 10 ⽉获得红杉资本投资的 Catalyst 。
假设你认为巴西⼲旱会导致咖啡减产，进⽽推⾼咖啡价格。
过去，对于⼀个普通投资者来说，即使这个判断是对的，也未必知道怎么赚钱。究竟应该买咖
啡期货，还是买相关公司的股票？什么时候建仓？买多少？判断错误后⼜该如何⽌损？
Catalyst 可以帮助投资者进⼀步研究这些问题，将投资想法转化为具体的交易策略，并完成程序
编写、回测以及⾃动交易。
这⼤幅降低了普通投资者参与复杂⾦融交易的⻔槛。
第⼆类，是帮专业投资机构寻找交易机会。
与第⼀类不同，这类公司的客⼾主要是对冲基⾦等专业投资机构。
它们的核⼼价值在于扩⼤投资研究的⼴度，让机构能够同时探索更多市场、资产和投资策略，
从中寻找超额收益。
⽐如今年 7 ⽉获得约 500 万美元融资的 KelAI ，可以接⼊基⾦内部的研究资料和私有数据库，让 AI
⾃主提出投资假设、编写代码并进⾏回测。
即使策略回测失败， AI 也会记录失败的原因。⽐如某个交易信号在美股有效，在其他市场却可能

失效。这些研究结果会被保留下来，成为 AI 下⼀轮研究的参考。
第三类，则是直接下场做投资。
今年 4 ⽉， AI 对冲基⾦ Abundance 披露获得了 1 亿美元的种⼦股权融资。
这家基⾦部署了数千个 AI Agent ，负责从互联⽹搜集信息、寻找投资机会、筛选做多和做空的股
票，到决定仓位⼤⼩、执⾏交易。⽬前，其部分选股策略已经完全由 AI 运⾏。
传统投资机构也在进⾏类似的尝试。 2024 年 7 ⽉，全球知名对冲基⾦桥⽔⾯向外部客⼾推出了⼀
只由 AI 主导投资决策的基⾦。
桥⽔将过去数⼗年积累的投资案例交给 AI ，让其学习宏观经济事件与资产价格之间的因果关系。
⽐如，美联储缩表会如何影响债券价格，美国提⾼关税⼜会对不同资产产⽣什么影响。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
211 收藏 分享到 :
在这只基⾦中，数据获取、⻛险控制和交易执⾏仍然由⼈类主导，但究竟是做多⽇元还是做空
股票，已经由 AI 来决定。
截⾄今年 9 ⽉底，这只基⾦的管理规模已经超过 45 亿美元，今年前三季度的收益率达到 16.4% ，
成⽴以来的年化收益率为 13.2% 。
从搭建交易策略，到寻找投资机会，再到直接管理资⾦， AI 正在接⼿过去需要专业投资团队才能
完成的⼤量⼯作。
总结
听起来， AI 似乎正在拉平散⼾与专业投资机构之间的能⼒差距。
过去，散⼾与专业机构之间的差距，很⼤程度上体现在信息获取和研究能⼒上。随着 AI 的普及，
这部分差距正在缩⼩。
但⾦融市场的特殊之处在于，投资能⼒的提升，并不意味着投资收益的提升。因为决定超额收
益的，是你相对于其他市场参与者的优势。
当所有⼈都能低成本地获取信息、分析财报、构建交易策略，这些能⼒本⾝的稀缺性也就下降
了。
真正值钱的，变成了 AI 背后那些⽆法被轻易复制的资源。⽐如，专业机构过去⼏⼗年积累的研究
笔记、专家访谈和交易经验，以及 42AI 能够接触到的近 3000 万名投资者的⾏为数据。
过去，受限于⼈⼒和技术，这些资源的价值远没有被充分挖掘。 AI 的出现，反⽽让机构有机会将
过去的资源积累转化为更⼤的竞争优势。
这可能才是 AI 时代散⼾⾯临的最⼤困境：⾃⼰的投资能⼒确实变强了，但对⼿变强的速度可能
更快。
AI 固然⼤幅增加了散⼾的研究能⼒，却也让超额收益变得稀缺。

Document 343: Downgrade of global equities to a Core allocation

Source PDF: all report pdf/Downgrade of global equities to a Core allocation.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

Downgrade of global 
equities to a Core allocation  
Summary 
Event: We are downgrading global equities from Overweight 
to a Neutral/Core allocation. We continue to maintain a 
Neutral allocation to fixed income, Overweight on Gold and 
Underweight on Cash. There are more details in our Global 
Market Outlook publication on 30 October 2026. 
What does the equity downgrade mean?  
This downgrade implies a slightly lower allocation to global 
equities within a diversified portfolio. For our CIO Foundation 
Balanced portfolio, this would imply an equity allocation of 
52.5%, down from 53.2%. We continue to expect positive 
returns for global equities in the coming 6-12 months, 
though equity investors may need to recalibrate expectations 
lower, compared with the 15-20% annualised gains 
experienced in the last few years. Our capital market 
assumptions still suggest a mid-to-high single digit annual 
returns over the next few years, consistent with a transition 
to a mid-cycle bull market. Consensus estimates point to 
16% earnings growth for global equities in 2027, which 
should overcome various headwinds to deliver positive 
returns for equities over the next 12 months. Investors should 
thus, continue to maintain exposure to equities within a 
diversified portfolio. 
In addition, we see a changing playbook to prepare for 
higher volatility, more active management and greater 
diversification, as discussed below. 
What does it mean for regional equities 
positioning? 
Within a global equities portfolio, we remain Overweight US 
and Asia ex-Japan (AxJ) equities, which are expected to 
deliver the highest earnings growth in 2027 amongst the 
major regions. This growth is led by the technology sector, 
which carries the largest weight in both regions. Within the 
diversified equities portfolio, we maintain Core allocations to

Standard Chartered Bank

Euro area equities, which offer lower valuations, and Japan 
equities, which is backed by a reflating economy. We are 
Underweight UK equities, where the sector composition 
offers lower growth prospects in 2027. Within the AxJ region, 
we are Overweight Taiwan equities, with attractive structural 
growth driven by its semiconductor industry. We are also 
Overweight India equities, which offers diversified non-tech 
growth drivers. Taiwan and India equities both generate 
attractive returns on equity. We maintain Core allocations to 
China equities, with low valuations, and Korea equities, with 
explosive earnings growth in its semiconductors industry, but 
volatile price movements. We are Underweight ASEAN, 
which offers relatively lower earnings growth. 
What are the implications for equity sectors? 
We are Overweight these sectors globally: US 
information technology, US healthcare, US financials, US 
materials, Europe ex-UK financials, and Japan 
financials. 
A framework we use is to break the market into four key 
sector building blocks. Tech is the largest block, 
encompassing the Information Technology, Communication 
Services and Consumer Discretionary sectors. Put 
differently, the segment spans five major industries: 
semiconductors, hardware, internet, software, and IT 
services. Within the US market, we expect this segment to 
deliver c.30% earnings growth in 2027, extending the strong 
momentum seen in 2026. 
Financials and Real Estate are the next building block, 
comprising these two sectors. We expect this segment to 
continue delivering low-teens earnings growth in 2027, 
supported by resilient economic activity and healthy credit 
trends. 
Resources include companies across four sectors: 
Industrials, Materials, Energy, and Utilities. We expect the 
segment to generate 10-15% earnings growth in 2027, 
although Energy is likely to remain the largest drag on overall 
growth due to tougher comparisons and softer energy price 
assumptions for 2027. 
Healthcare & Others consists primarily of Healthcare and 
Consumer Staples. We expect mid-teen earnings growth for 
the segment in 2027, with a rebound in healthcare earnings 
representing a potential upside surprise. 
Our Overweight sector exposures are drawn from each of the 
four key blocks above. Unlike the last few years, with 
concentrated gains from Tech providing market leadership, 
we see our downgrade in global equities also reflecting a 
broadening in market leadership towards each of the other 
sector blocks apart from Tech.

As structural themes such as AI transition from the rapid 
buildout phase towards monetisation, diversified exposure 
across sectors and regions should become increasingly 
important. 
What are the implications for equity 
opportunistic ideas? 
We have opportunistic ideas on Global High-Dividend 
equities, US healthcare, Euro area banks, MSCI 
Switzerland and Japan banks. The selection represents a 
more defensive stance currently, with an average beta of 
below 1 relative to global equities (MSCI AC World), but 
attractive drivers nonetheless, for each of the ideas.  
Global High-Dividend offers higher-income equity allocation 
with historically lower volatility than global equities. US 
healthcare is supported by wider adoption of obesity 
treatments, advances in oncology and innovative medical 
devices, that support earnings growth. Euro area banks 
continue to see steady earnings growth adding to their 
excess capital, which is being returned to shareholders via 
attractive dividend and share buyback yields. Japan banks 
continue to enjoy net margin expansion from BoJ policy 
normalisation, as well as corporate governance 
improvements. MSCI Switzerland is a newly initiated idea for 
investors seeking defensive exposure. Healthcare and 
consumer staples accounts for more than half the index 
weight, providing exposure to relatively resilient demand that 
is more insulated from economic cycles and AI trends. It also 
has a sizeable financial sector supported by steady wealth 
management growth and well-capitalised insurers with 
disciplined underwriting and resilient premium income. 
We see the case for more active management in a mid-
cycle transition, as higher market dispersion should create 
greater opportunities for active sector and stock selection, 
particularly as leadership within equities evolves. 
What are the implications of higher equity 
market volatility?  
A more uncertain political backdrop and potentially maturing 
growth rates in certain market segments may contribute to 
more frequent bouts of market volatility. Higher volatility 
presents an opportunity to use structured strategies to gain 
exposure to equities. Such strategies may be used to 
generate income or to gain exposure at a discounted entry 
price. Investors can also consider strategies that are 
structured to return principal or partial principal at maturity. 
What alternative strategies are suited to a 
regime of moderating equity market returns?  
Investors can consider alternative strategies such as hedge 
fund strategies or private markets, for those with the capacity

to accept some illiquidity.  
Equity long/short hedge fund strategies would retain growth 
participation with less market exposure. Fund managers buy 
companies they expect to outperform and short those they 
expect to underperform, seeking to earn the difference 
between the two, and benefit even as total equity market 
returns are moderating. 
Within private markets, private equity provides a means to 
higher returns with the effects of an illiquidity premium. 
Meanwhile, infrastructure offers differentiated exposure to 
structural growth opportunities, where contracted long-term 
revenues reduce dependence on equity-market sentiment. 
What are the implications on fixed income 
and FX markets? 
Bonds: This year’s surge in bond yields means bonds now 
offer a hedge against any equity market weakness. We 
believe the 3-7-year part of the US bond yield curve offers 
the best hedge against any equity sell-off caused by AI-
related growth worries. This part of the bond curve has priced 
in most Fed rate hike expectations, which could reverse if 
growth concerns return. Meanwhile, we favour US Treasury 
Inflation-Protected Securities (TIPS) as a hedge against any 
fuel price-led inflation scare.  
Credit: For corporate bonds, adopting a defensive stance in 
a volatile environment typically involves: i) increasing credit 
quality, favouring higher-rated issuers; and ii) moving up the 
capital structure, by reducing exposure to subordinated debt 
and favouring secured debt. From a sector perspective, 
large, diversified banks, utilities, and quasi-sovereigns tend 
to be more defensive, particularly relative to cyclical sectors, 
such as metals and mining, exploration, and technology.         
FX: We favour bearish AUD/JPY as a tactical hedge against 
any equity market correction. Review of 21 past S&P500 
correction sessions (2010-2025) shows AUD weakened 
against USD in 86% of cases, while JPY strengthened 
against USD in 67%, supporting our bearish AUD/JPY 
outlook during any equity market stress.

Document 403: Meta将Muse智能体接入Instagram与沃尔玛

Source PDF: all report pdf/Meta将Muse智能体接入Instagram与沃尔玛.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-25
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

沃尔玛与 Meta 达成合作，⽤⼾可通过 AI 助⼿ Muse 搜索⿊五促销商品、⽐较价格并完成下
单，⽆需浏览沃尔玛⽹站。合作由沃尔玛 Agent Gateway 系统⽀持，该系统将向更多 AI 平
台开放。与此同时，沃尔玛持续升级⾃有助⼿ Sparky ，将 AI 功能延伸⾄线上购物和线下⻔
店导航。
摘要：
沃尔玛正尝试让消费者通过 AI 助⼿直接完成购物。当地时间周⼆，沃尔玛宣布与 Meta 达成合
作，⽤⼾可以通过 Meta 旗下 AI 智能体 Muse 购买沃尔玛的⿊⾊星期五促销商品，⽆需⾃⾏浏览沃
尔玛⽹站，即可完成商品搜索、⽐价和下单。
具体⽽⾔，⽤⼾打开 Muse 应⽤并登录沃尔玛账号后，只需描述所需商品， Muse 便会向沃尔玛
发送请求，由沃尔玛返回相关商品信息，帮助⽤⼾⽐较选项、寻找优惠并⽣成购物⻋。
沃尔玛表⽰，合作体验内置了隐私保护机制。Muse ⽆法访问⽤⼾的沃尔玛购物历史及其他个⼈
信息；消费者可以设置消费限额、要求购买前审批，并查看 AI 代理提交的订单，也可以随时删

除代理档案。
此次合作意味着沃尔玛开始通过外部 AI 助⼿拓展购物⼊⼝。⽤⼾不再必须进⼊零售商⾃有⽹站搜
索商品，⽽是可以将购物需求交给 AI 代理处理。
开放 Agent Gateway ，沃尔玛争夺 AI 购物新⼊⼝
此次合作由沃尔玛今年 10 ⽉上线的代理⽹关系统（ Agent Gateway ）提供⽀持。该系统允许外
部 AI 助⼿直接连接沃尔玛线上商店，获取商品信息并协助消费者购物。
沃尔玛计划向更多开发者和 AI 平台开放 Agent Gateway 。这意味着未来接⼊沃尔玛的 AI 助⼿可能
不⽌ Muse ，更多外部平台也有机会通过这⼀系统提供购物服务。 Instacart 近期也与 Meta 旗下
Muse 达成了合作。
沃尔玛与亚⻢逊在这⼀领域采取了不同策略。亚⻢逊已采取措施阻⽌第三⽅ AI 代理代替消费者完
成购买，⽽沃尔玛选择主动开放零售接⼝，允许外部 AI 助⼿参与商品搜索和交易流程。
两种路线反映出零售商对 AI 时代电商⼊⼝的不同判断：亚⻢逊倾向于维护⾃⾝平台的交易闭环，

沃尔玛则希望借助外部 AI 助⼿扩⼤商品触达范围，将其转化为新的购物渠道。
升级⾃有助⼿ Sparky ， AI 延伸⾄线下购物
在与外部 AI 平台合作的同时，沃尔玛也在升级⾃有购物助⼿ Sparky ，将 AI 应⽤延伸⾄实体⻔
店。
近期推出的新功能包括将⼿写购物清单转化为购物⻋、通过照⽚和语⾳指令搜索商品，以及帮
助消费者在⻔店内定位商品。⽤⼾搜索商品后，可以通过⻔店地图查看其所在货架； “ 闪现价签 ”
（ Flash Price Tag ）功能还可以让⽬标商品的货架价签亮起，⽅便消费者寻找。
从 Muse 接⼊外部购物场景，到 Sparky 覆盖线上搜索和线下寻货，沃尔玛正在将 AI 嵌⼊消费者购
物的不同环节。随着 Agent Gateway 向更多平台开放，外部 AI 助⼿能否为沃尔玛带来更多实际订
单，以及 AI 代理是否会逐渐成为电商购物的重要⼊⼝，将成为后续值得关注的⽅向。

Document 417: Productivity_ reasons for optimism (2026-11-25)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Productivity_ reasons for optimism (2026-11-25).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 15
Status: ok

### Page 1

#gdp #australia #employment #housing #rba #productivity
Productivity: reasons for optimism
By Pat Bustamante
Senior Economist, Westpac
08:37 November 17 2026 Share
The supply-side outlook is improving as capital deepening strengthens, reallocation drags
fade and AI diffusion gathers pace.
The supply-side outlook is looking brighter. A pickup in capital deepening and a fading ‘reallocation’
drag are already evident in the Annual National Accounts, with AI diffusion offering further upside.

This gives us greater conviction that productivity growth can return towards 1% or more over time,

### Page 2

Growth in 2024-25 was a little firmer than first thought
The 2025–26 figures show somewhat firmer economic activity and business investment than previously
estimated, particularly in 2024–25. The economy grew by 2.3% in year-average terms, broadly in line
with the initial quarterly National Accounts estimate of 2.4%. However, growth in 2024–25 was revised
up by 0.3ppts to 1.6%, largely reflecting stronger private business investment. Business investment is

now estimated to have increased by 1.1% in 2024–25, compared with the initial estimate of a 0.2%
decline. The revisions do not materially alter the broader productivity story, but they do suggest that the
economy was growing a little more strongly for the same rate of inflation in 2025.
above the RBA’s assumption and closer to Treasury’s. The recovery illustrates why it is important to
focus on longer-term trends in productivity growth rather than on the latest year or two, which can be

volatile.
Capital deepening is returning. Investment is picking up and, outside mining, market-sector capital
per hour worked is now at a record high, rising a strong 1.5% in 2025–26.
The reallocation headwind is easing. The shift of labour towards non-market industries, which
generally have lower measured productivity levels, has reduced the level of aggregate productivity by
around 1ppt over the past 15 years. With the expansion of the non-market sector plateauing, this

drag looks set to fade.
Productivity growth is about more than capital. Multifactor productivity, which captures how efficiently
businesses combine labour and capital, was the dominant driver of market-sector productivity growth
in 2025–26. Meanwhile, stronger investment in IT, utilities and professional services should provide a
further lift as new capital is put to work.

### Page 3

The investment upswing is broadening
The Annuals also provide a richer picture of the supply side, including investment by industry and the
evolution of the capital stock, details that are not available in the quarterly accounts. These data point
to a notable lift in investment in the market (ex-mining) part of the economy, where investment has
gone from around 10% of nominal GDP in to just over 11.5%. Outside of this, investment has been
softer, particularly in the mining sector.

### Page 4

Within the market sector, investment by the IT industry, increasingly focused on data centres,
has risen to its highest share of nominal GDP since the technology boom of the 1990s. Utilities
investment, including renewables and associated infrastructure, is stronger still, at its highest share
since 1983, while investment by the professional services industry has reached a record high.

### Page 5

Across the non-market sector (including health, education and public administration), investment has
also held at a higher share of the economy, driven by the health and public administration (including
defence) industries.

### Page 6

Investment is translating into capital deepening
The investment upswing is also beginning to translate into capital deepening as industries look to
rebuild capital stocks after a period of soft growth. Outside mining, market-sector capital per hour
worked is at a record high and well above its pre-pandemic level. That is an encouraging
development, particularly given that the sector accounts for 70% of total employment.
There are important divergences beneath the aggregate. Mining continues to experience capital

shallowing, as investment has been insufficient to cover wear and tear and capital requirements
associated with additional workers. By contrast, the capital stock in the non-market sector appears to
have caught up with the surge in employment we have seen since the pandemic, with capital per
worker increasing for the first time since the pandemic.

### Page 7

A major productivity headwind is starting to fade
Capital deepening will help support productivity growth across the economy. The Annuals show that
productivity in the market sector (excluding mining) continues to grow by around 1% in year-average
terms, while mining remains a drag on aggregate productivity (down 0.8% in 2025–26 and almost 30%
since 2017).

### Page 8

At the same time, a major recent headwind is easing as the rapid expansion of the non-market sector
approaches slows. This matters for aggregate productivity. Productivity growth reflects both gains
within industries, through capital deepening, technology and better processes, and the ‘reallocation’ of
resources between industries with different productivity levels.
Measured productivity in the non-market sector is considerably lower than across much of the market

economy, partly reflecting measurement issues (output in these sectors is generally not well measured,
particularly when there are quality changes). As employment shifted towards these industries, this
compositional effect weighed on aggregate productivity. Our estimates suggest it has reduced the
level of aggregate productivity by a significant 1ppt over the past 15 years and almost 0.3ppts
over the three years to 2025–26.

### Page 9

Productivity gains were about more than capital
It wasn’t only capital deepening that drove productivity growth. Multifactor productivity (MFP), which
captures how efficiently businesses combine labour and capital through changes in processes,
technology, know-how, was the dominant driver of market-sector productivity growth this year. This
gives us more conviction that the supply side is normalising, and productivity growth will improve from
its recent lows.

### Page 10

Where productivity is improving, and where it isn’t
At the industry level, some of those recording stronger investment are also beginning to deliver
stronger productivity outcomes, although the picture remains uneven.
IT stands out at the positive end of the spectrum, alongside improving outcomes in manufacturing,
administrative and support services, and transport.. These are also industries where the diffusion of
AI and other digital technologies could deliver further productivity gains. Utilities and

professional services should also benefit as stronger investment translates into greater capital intensity
and efficiency.
At the other end, mining remains a major drag, consistent with the capital shallowing evident in the
investment and capital-stock data. Construction is another notable laggard, with productivity continuing
to deteriorate despite strong demand from housing, population growth, renewable-energy infrastructure
and data-centre projects. Construction productivity fell around 2% in 2025–26 and almost 21% over the

13 years from 2012–13 to 2025–26.

### Page 11

A more constructive supply-side story
The Annuals provide a more constructive supply-side picture than the aggregate productivity figures
alone suggest. Market-sector productivity outside mining continues to advance, the reallocation drag
from the expanding non-market sector looks likely to fade, and capital deepening is strengthening
across important parts of the economy. The strong MFP outcome in the market sector gives us more
conviction that the supply side is normalising, and productivity growth will improve going ahead.

The critical question is whether the strong investment underway in technology, professional services,
electricity and related infrastructure translates into stronger productivity growth as that new capital is
put to work. If it does, the improvement in capital intensity evident in the Annuals could mark the
beginning of a more durable lift in Australia’s productivity performance.

### Page 12

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics: November 2026: Key events, themes and trends to watch in the month ahead |  November 2026
US-China talks, a possible Trump-Xi-Putin trilateral meeting, and any signs of a China-Japan diplomatic
thaw will be critical watchpoints. The priorities of the summit between representatives of 21 Asia-Pacific
economies are set to include efforts to boost innovation, digital connectivity, supply-chain resilience and

support for an open multilateral trading system. During the summit, presidents Trump and Xi are expected to
meet for the third time this year. This meeting will provide signals on the state of China-US talks, including the
operationalisation of new bilateral trade and investment boards, and on the likelihood of another extension of
the Busan trade truce beyond January 2027. President Trump has also indicated he is open to a trilateral US-
China-Russia meeting during the summit, possibly covering nuclear arms control and the Russia-Ukraine war.

Investors will also closely watch whether Japanese Prime Minister Takaichi and President Xi meet on the
sidelines, following reports that Tokyo is hoping to arrange a meeting. However, the bar for a formal
breakthrough will likely remain high. As we noted in a report earlier this year, a full normalisation of relations
and an easing of Chinese economic restrictions – which include rare-earth export controls – may require a
lengthy period of diplomatic re-engagement and backchannel diplomacy.

A divided government could mean less change at home but more abroad. Third-party polling and betting
markets forecast Democrat control of the House, and an increasing probability of flipping the Senate. 80% of
respondents in our monthly poll now say living costs will influence their vote – the most since April. For
markets, a divided government could mean less domestic legislative change, but not necessarily a more
predictable US foreign policy. Losing Congress could leave theadministration’s geopolitical and geoeconomic

toolkit largely intact.
In our view, weapons stockpiles, inflationary pressures and long-end yields may matter more than Congress
in constraining theadministration’s choices in 2027 and 2028. A Democratic House could dial up oversight of
the administration, launch investigations, force votes on War Powers Resolutions, and use appropriations bills
to press for restrictions on departmental funding. This may generate a steady stream of attention-grabbing

headlines, but whether it decisively affects the event-driven risk that matters for markets (e.g. blocking a tariff
or preventing a limited military strike) remains in doubt. If domestic policymaking becomes more challenging,
foreign policy could become a top priority for the Trump administration.
In the spotlight for November 2026: US midterms and APEC Summit

### Page 4

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics: November 2026: Key events, themes and trends to watch in the month ahead |  November 2026
NewZealand’s general election is shaping up as a close and fragmented contest. Both the ruling National
and opposition Labour parties are polling at historically low levels, and smaller parties will likely determine
the next government. In a closely contested vote, investors could see coalition uncertainty and a wider

range of possible outcomes for tax, spending, infrastructure, energy and migration policy.
The National party is campaigning on a platform that would broadly preserve the current emphasis on
fiscal restraint, lower regulation and private-sector investment. A Labour-led government could place
greater weight on public services, housing and climate policy, alongside a proposed capital-gains tax on
investment property. However, because any government will rely on minor parties for survival, the final

policy mix will depend heavily on post-election negotiations.
The meeting will be a test of the EU’s ability to translate the competitiveness agenda into practical
reforms. The focus will be on Europe’s ability to restore competitiveness, mobilize investment and
maintain fiscal discipline. The Eurogroup brings together Euro-area finance ministers and is expected to
consider macroeconomic and fiscal developments, energy market pressures and progress towards the

Savings and Investments Union. ECOFIN brings together all EU finance ministers, and will focus on
financial integration, banking sector competitiveness, and economic coordination. To watch will be
tensions between fiscal consolidation and the need to fund defence, energy security and strategic
investment, alongside progress on integrating Europe’s fragmented capital markets and banking sectors. A
budget-focused Council meeting follows on 10 November.

Implementation of existing commitments, rather than new policy pledges, is expected to be the primary
focus of the next climate summit in Antalya. Building on the shift towards implementation at COP30,
COP31 will focus on translating existing climate commitments into practical action, including through
investment, financing and partnerships. For investors, the key watchpoint will be whether the summit can
deliver tangible commitments that translate existing plans into binding funding pledges for critical

infrastructure resilience, scaled-up renewable energy output, and climate finance.

### Page 5

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics: November 2026: Key events, themes and trends to watch in the month ahead |  November 2026
Melaka’s state election will offer a further gauge of Malaysia’s evolving electoral landscape after
strong recent state-level performances by Barisan Nasional (BN). The contest will also be watched for
its read-through to Prime Minister Anwar Ibrahim’s Pakatan Harapan (PH), following setbacks in other

state elections this year.
BN enters the election from a position of relative strength, while its electoral understanding with PAS
and its allies could provide an early indication of how political alignments are evolving ahead of the next
general election. However, Melaka’s local dynamics mean the result should not be treated as a direct
national forecast.
The anticipated signing of a landmark digital economy agreement is set to be one of the most

significant summit deliverables in years. The 49th ASEAN Summit in Manila will focus on economic
integration, energy security, supply-chain resilience, maritime security, the crisis in Myanmar, and
relations with external partners.
For markets, the formal signing of the ASEAN Digital Economy Framework Agreement (DEFA) is the key
event to watch. Designed as a legally binding treaty, DEFA aims to harmonize regional digital trade rules
and cross-border data flows. Successful implementation is expected to double the projected size of the

region’s digital economy by 2030, boosting it from a projected baseline of US$1 trillion to US$2 trillion
(up from approximately US$300 billion today).
The second EU-UK Summit will be an important test of whether the post-Brexit relationship can move
towards tangible outcomes. Prime Minister Andy Burnham will seek to finalize agreements on food and
agricultural standards, youth mobility, and linking emissions trading systems. Burnham has framed
relations as“not good enough.” To watch will be whether we see concrete deals versus declarations, and

whether leaders are prepared to make the concessions necessary to deepen ties.

### Page 6

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics: November 2026: Key events, themes and trends to watch in the month ahead |  November 2026
The upcoming local elections could serve as a political barometer ahead of the 2028 general election–
but only up to a point. Local elections in Taiwan are primarily driven by municipal ‘bread and butter’
economic issues; geopolitics and cross-Strait relations have historically not been at the forefront of

voters’ concerns. While this divergence limits theelections’ predictive value for the January 2028 general
vote, the results can provide a sense of the domestic balance of power.
Elections in Taiwan’s six special municipalities – Taipei, New Taipei, Taichung, Tainan, Taoyuan, and
Kaohsiung – will serve as particularly important watchpoints. Some recent polling suggests a potentially
close contest in Taipei between incumbent mayor Chiang Wan-an (Kuomintang) and legislator Shen Po-

yang (Democratic Progressive Party).
Spain’s snap general election could mark a major turning point for Spanish politics and the EU,
potentially ending eight years of Socialist-led government under Pedro Sánchez and bringing the
conservative People’s Party (PP) to power with support from the far-right Vox. Polling suggests PP is
ahead and a PP-Vox alignment is the only one projected to reach a majority. A rightward shift in one of
the EU’s largest economies could occur just as Europe enters a wider electoral cycle, potentially

complicating consensus on migration, climate policy, Ukraine and European integration.
The Forum comes at a pivotal moment in EU-Latin American relations. It is the EU’s first big commercial
push into Argentina since the EU-Mercosur trade deal began to be provisionally applied on 1 May. The
agreement opens new opportunities for trade, investment and market access. The EU is Argentina’s
largest source of foreign direct investment. Around 800 government, business and financial leaders will

focus on energy, critical minerals, agriculture and infrastructure. The EU’s Global Gateway investment
agenda provides a framework for mobilizing public and private capital. While a key outcome will be the
launch of a structured EU-Argentina investment dialogue, to watch will be the ability of Argentine
officials to allay concerns around policy stability and regulatory predictability.

### Page 7

Helen Belopolsky, Miha Hribernik | This Month in Geopolitics: November 2026: Key events, themes and trends to watch in the month ahead |  November 2026
A potential Trump-Kim meeting in November could restart official bilateral dialogue, though North
Korea remains highly unlikely to abandon its nuclear program. South Korea’s National Intelligence
Service said in late September that it saw a roughly 50% chance of US-North Korea talks around the
APEC Summit in Shenzhen, although more recent reporting suggests the prospects may have

diminished. If talks do resume, they would only mark the first step in a long and highly uncertain
diplomatic process.
As we noted earlier this year, North Korea is highly unlikely to agree to denuclearization. Unless renewed
negotiations sidestep denuclearization in favor of a risk-reduction or coexistence framework, dialogue is
likely to stall, not least given Pyongyang’s constitutional pledge to continue expanding its nuclear
arsenal. This would make even a negotiated freeze on further expansion difficult to achieve.

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 20 日

調査レポート

コモディティ・レポート（2026 年 7～9 月）

調査部 主任研究員 芥田 知至 
1. コモディティ市況全般：原油が上昇傾向、銅は最高値更新、金は上値重い 
ドル建て国際商品市況全般の動向を示す FTSE・コアコモディティ CRB 指数は、2023 年 5 月 31 日に 2022
年 1 月以来の安値をつけた後、2026 年 3～9 月には大幅に上昇して 9 月 10 日に 2008 年 7 月以来の高値を
つけた。その後、10 月にかけてやや下落した。コモディティ相場は、中東情勢や米金融政策の行方を巡って、引
き続き、不安定な推移が見こまれる。

2. エネルギー市況：足元はブレントが一時 110 ドル、WTI が 100 ドル前後 
国際指標のブレント原油は、2026 年 4 月 30 日に 126.41 ドルと 2022 年 3 月以来の高値をつけた。足元は
110 ドル前後で不安定な動きとなっている。米国とイランは戦闘終結やホルムズ海峡再開に向けた動きは停滞し
たままであり、緊迫する中東情勢を受けて原油は高止まりが見こまれる。

3. ベースメタル市況：銅は 9 月上旬に 14,875 ドルの史上最高値 
銅相場は、2026 年 9 月 10 日に 14,875 ドルと史上最高値を記録し、足元は 14,000 ドル台半ばを中心に推
移している。銅は、原油高や景気悪化の影響が懸念される一方で、AI・エネルギー転換・防衛の関連分野を中
心に需要が伸びるとの見方が根強く、高値圏での一進一退が見こまれる。

4. 貴金属市況：金は 8 月中旬に 4,700 ドル近くまで持ち直すも足元は 4,100 ドル前後 
金は、2026 年 1 月 29 日に 5,594.82 ドルと史上最高値を記録した後、6 月 30 日には 3,942.99 ドルまで下
落し、足元は 4,100 ドル前後を中心に推移している。米利上げが続くとの観測が有力な中、中東情勢も安定化に
はまだ遠く、原油高が意識されて、金は買われにくい状況が続きやすいとみられる。

原油・銅・金相場の騰落率（2026 年 9 月末の 2026 年 6 月末との対比）

1. コモディティ市況全般の概況：原油が上昇傾向、銅は最高値更新、金は上値重い 
ドル建て国際商品市況全般の動向を示す FTSE・コアコモディティ CRB 指数（終値ベース）は、2022 年 6 月
9 日に 2011 年 9 月以来の高値をつけた後、2023 年 5 月 31 日には 2022 年 1 月以来の安値をつけた。2026
年 3～9 月に大幅に上昇し、9 月 10 日に 2008 年 7 月以来の高値をつけた。10 月にかけてやや下落した。 
マクロ経済環境をみると、フーシ派とサウジアラビアによる攻撃の応酬が激しさを増すなど中東の混乱に広がり
がみられ、米国とイランも戦闘終結やホルムズ海峡再開へ向けた動きは停滞したままである。原油は緊迫する中
東情勢を受けて上昇傾向で推移した。金は、原油高などを受けた根強い米利上げ観測を背景に上値が重い状
況が続いた。銅は、原油高や景気悪化が懸念される一方で、AI・エネルギー転換・防衛の関連分野を中心に需
要が伸びるとの見方が根強く、史上最高値圏で一進一退となった。中東情勢や米金融政策を巡って、コモディテ
ィ相場は不安定な推移が続くと見こまれる。
